Additionally, we design a generative large-model-driven dynamic semantic-adaptive decoder at the receiver, which can adapt to videos of arbitrary lengths. Simulation results demonstrate that LGVSC significantly outperforms traditional schemes, achieving a channel bandwidth ratio on the order of 10^-4 to 10^-3, while maintaining strong zero-shot generalization across downstream tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 受万物互联中海量视频传输需求的驱动，语义通信在平衡传输效率与质量方面展现出巨大潜力。本文提出一种大模型驱动的生成式视频语义通信（LGVSC）框架，能够在极低带宽条件下实现高效的视频语义传输。首先，通过解耦编码器与解码器并暴露显式中间语义表示，LGVSC保持了可解释性，避免了端到端系统中常见的黑盒行为。其次，我们引入了一种新的度量——基于概率的语义相似度评分（PSSS），能够在连续范围内量化复杂模态的语义相似度，从而实现对语义内容更精确的评估。基于PSSS，我们提出了一种由多模态大模型驱动的语义引导关键帧提取模块，该模块可在发送端增强关键帧选择过程中的细粒度语义一致性，在不牺牲语义保真度的前提下优化传输带宽。此外，我们还在接收端设计了一种生成式大模型驱动的动态语义自适应解码器，能够适应任意长度的视频。仿真结果表明，LGVSC显著优于传统方案，信道带宽比达到10^-4至10^-3量级，且在下游任务上保持强大的零样本泛化能力。

</details>


### [87] [Simulating Torsional Vibrations of Faulty Bevel Gears Using the Polygonal Contact Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12967)
*Milla Vehviläinen,Aleksanteri Hämäläinen,Pekka Rahkola,Mikko Savolainen,Janne Keränen,Jari Halme,Jenni Pippuri-Mäkeläinen,Kari Tammi,Anouar Belahcen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于先进多边形接触方法的多体仿真技术，用于复现方位推力器实验台上的扭振信号，能够模拟健康与任意故障几何形状的齿轮，仿真信号在时域和频域上与实测数据高度一致，为数据驱动的状态监测方法提供了坚实基础。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 机电应用中齿轮的准确状态监测依赖高质量故障数据，但故障数据稀缺。为解决数据不足的问题，提出了一种能够模拟任意故障几何形状齿轮的仿真方法。

Method: 采用基于先进多边形接触方法的多体仿真，复现实验方位推力器实验台的扭振信号，支持健康齿轮和任意故障几何形状齿轮的模拟。

Result: 仿真信号在时域和频域上与实测数据高度匹配。时域上平均扭矩水平和周期性波动吻合良好，但实测信号峰值幅度更高、噪声更大；频域上准确再现了预期的故障频率及边带，故障越大幅度越高。仿真倾向于高估峰值幅度并低估外部噪声，但整体结果与实测高度可比且符合物理预期。

Conclusion: 该仿真方法为增强数据驱动的状态监测方法（特别是基于机器学习或深度学习的方法）提供了坚实基础。

Original Abstract: Gears are an integral component of electromechanical applications, but accurate condition monitoring methods, including data-driven predictive maintenance, are strongly dependent on high-quality data, especially from faulty components. To address the scarcity of data, we proposed a multibody simulation using an advanced polygonal contact method to replicate torsional vibrations from an experimental azimuth thruster test rig. The key novelty is the ability to simulate both healthy and faulty gears with arbitrary fault geometries. The simulated signals closely matched the measurements in both time and frequency domains. In the time domain, average torque levels and periodic fluctuations aligned well, although measured signals exhibited higher peak-to-peak amplitudes and greater noise, particularly in healthy conditions at lower rotational speeds. In the frequency domain, the simulations accurately reproduced expected fault frequencies and corresponding sidebands, with larger faults producing higher amplitudes. While the simulations tended to overestimate peak amplitudes and underestimate external noise, the results were highly comparable to measurements and consistent with the physical expectations. These findings provide a robust foundation for enhancing data-driven condition monitoring methods, particularly those employing machine learning or deep learning.

中文摘要翻译: 齿轮是机电应用中不可或缺的组成部分，但准确的状态监测方法（包括数据驱动的预测性维护）高度依赖于高质量数据，尤其是来自故障部件的数据。为解决数据稀缺问题，我们提出了一种使用先进多边形接触方法的多体仿真，用于复现实验方位推力器实验台上的扭振信号。关键创新在于能够模拟具有任意故障几何形状的健康齿轮和故障齿轮。仿真信号在时域和频域上与实测信号高度吻合。在时域上，平均扭矩水平和周期性波动吻合良好，尽管实测信号表现出更高的峰峰值幅度和更大的噪声，尤其是在较低转速的健康工况下。在频域上，仿真准确再现了预期的故障频率及相应的边带，且故障越大幅度越高。虽然仿真倾向于高估峰值幅度并低估外部噪声，但结果与实测高度可比且符合物理预期。这些发现为增强数据驱动的状态监测方法（特别是采用机器学习或深度学习的方法）提供了坚实基础。

</details>


### [88] [Thermal characterisation by Scanning Photothermal Radiometry using a random undersampled measurement scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13046)
*Florian Crouau,Alejandro Mateos-Canseco,Jérémie Maire,Jean-Luc Battaglia,Stéphane Chevalier*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出在扫描光热辐射测量（SPR）中采用不规则采样和加权随机技术，在碳纤维增强铝基复合材料样本上实现测量量减少6倍，同时保持成像质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SPR虽然具有高时空分辨率和非破坏性等优点，但逐点扫描方式耗时过长，需要在不牺牲太多信息的前提下大幅减少测量次数以提高效率。

Method: 采用稀疏信号上的不规则采样（irregular sampling）结合加权随机技术（weighted random technique），减少所需的测量样本数量。

Result: 在碳纤维/铝基复合材料样本上，成功将测量量减少6倍，同时保持了SPR技术的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过不规则采样和加权随机方法，可以显著降低SPR的测量时间开销，在不影响主要信息获取的前提下提升该技术的实用性。

Original Abstract: Scanning Photothermal Radiometry (SPR) is an active thermal technique that is simultaneously non-destructive, contactless, and allows for temporal resolutions on the order of nanoseconds, spatial resolutions down to the sub-micrometre scale and at different depths. This scanning method can be time consuming thus this work shows that it is possible to reduce the amount of measurements taken by 6 when using SPR on a sample consisting of carbon fibres in an aluminium matrix. It uses irregular sampling on sparse signals, and a weighted random technique to further decrease the amount of samples needed.

中文摘要翻译: 扫描光热辐射测量（SPR）是一种主动热学技术，具有非破坏性、非接触式、纳秒级时间分辨率、亚微米级空间分辨率以及不同深度探测能力。该扫描方法可能较为耗时，因此本工作表明，当对碳纤维增强铝基复合材料样本使用SPR时，可以将测量量减少6倍。该方法采用稀疏信号上的不规则采样，并结合加权随机技术进一步减少所需的样本数量。

</details>


### [89] [Inverse Learning assisted V2I Communication for Intent Based 6G ISAC Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13091)
*Anoop C,Anup Aprem*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对6G车联网中融合ISAC与意图驱动网络范式的RSU，提出基于逆强化学习的V2I通信策略推断框架，包括三种非参数/参数化方法及联邦学习扩展，并在车辆微云的两个应用场景中验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 6G将引入意图驱动网络和通感一体化（ISAC）能力，使RSU成为自主认知智能体。由于RSU的决策策略未知且动态变化，V2I通信策略需要学习和适应RSU的自主决策行为，因此需要从观测到的RSU行为中逆向推断其潜在意图（效用函数）。

Method: (i) ATIL：基于Afriat定理的非参数固定效用学习方法；(ii) FICNNIL：使用全输入凹神经网络的参数化固定效用学习方法；(iii) PICNNIL：基于部分输入凹神经网络的参数化状态相关效用学习方法；(iv) FedFICNNIL和FedPICNNIL：面向固定效用和状态相关效用的联邦逆学习算法。

Result: 在车辆微云（VMC）的两个V2I通信应用（预测性调度协同数据下载与动态簇头选择）中验证了所提逆学习框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了一套完整的从观测RSU行为中推断其意图（效用函数）的逆学习框架，涵盖了非参数、参数化及联邦学习多种场景，为6G意图驱动车联网中V2I通信策略的自适应学习提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: 6G is expected to bring unprecedented advancements in the capabilities of vehicular networks. However, the advent of 6G will also introduce changes in the operation of vehicular communication infrastructures such as roadside units (RSUs), including the incorporation of autonomous intent-based network paradigm and integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) capabilities. While ISAC enables sensing and communication within a single 6G network node, intent-based network design paradigm ensures that network nodes such as RSUs, act as autonomous cognitive agents to fulfill the objectives of their respective communication service providers. This paradigm shift necessitates the development of V2I communication strategies that learns and adapts to the sensing-assisted communication and the autonomous decision-making strategies of RSUs. We model the RSU as a constrained utility maximizer, where the utility function characterizes the RSU intent, and formulate an inverse learning (IL) problem to infer the underlying utility function from observed ISAC RSU actions, for example the adaptive beamwidth allocation in response to the kinematic states of vehicles within a vehicular micro-cloud (VMC). The main contributions of this paper are: (i) ATIL, a nonparametric method based on Afriat theorem for fixed utility learning; (ii) FICNNIL, a parametric approach using fully input-concave neural networks, for structured fixed utility learning; and (iii) PICNNIL, a parametric approach based on partially input-concave neural networks, for inverse learning of state-dependent utilities. (iv) Federated inverse learning algorithms FedFICNNIL and FedPICNNIL for fixed and state dependent utility, respectively. We demonstrate the proposed IL-based framework for two V2I communication applications in VMCs, namely predictive scheduling for cooperative data downloading and dynamic cluster-head selection.

中文摘要翻译: 6G有望为车联网带来前所未有的能力提升。然而，6G的到来也将改变路边单元（RSU）等车辆通信基础设施的运作方式，包括融入自主意图驱动的网络范式以及通感一体化（ISAC）能力。ISAC使得单个6G网络节点能够同时进行感知与通信，而意图驱动的网络设计范式确保RSU等网络节点作为自主认知智能体，以满足各自通信服务提供商的业务目标。这一范式转变要求开发能够学习并适应RSU的感知辅助通信行为和自主决策策略的V2I通信策略。本文将RSU建模为一个带约束的效用最大化器，其中效用函数表征RSU的意图，并构建了一个逆学习（IL）问题，从观测到的ISAC RSU行为（例如，根据车辆微云（VMC）内车辆的运动状态自适应调整波束宽度分配）中推断潜在的效用函数。本文的主要贡献包括：(i) ATIL，一种基于Afriat定理的非参数固定效用学习方法；(ii) FICNNIL，一种基于全输入凹神经网络的参数化结构化固定效用学习方法；(iii) PICNNIL，一种基于部分输入凹神经网络的参数化状态相关效用逆学习方法；以及(iv) FedFICNNIL和FedPICNNIL，分别面向固定效用和状态相关效用的联邦逆学习算法。我们在VMC中的两个V2I通信应用上展示了所提出的逆学习框架的有效性，即预测性调度协同数据下载和动态簇头选择。

</details>


### [90] [A High Input Impedance Chopper Stabilized Amplifier Based On Charge Conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13129)
*Prabhas K Deshpande,Naveen Kadayinti*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种差分电容翻转技术，用于斩波稳定型电容耦合仪表放大器（CCIA），以提升其输入阻抗。该技术通过在每个周期重新配置电容位置，避免电容的充放电，使输入阻抗变为纯电容性且与斩波频率无关，从而在干电极ECG信号采集等应用中实现高达21 GΩ的直流输入阻抗，功耗仅2.6 μW。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 斩波稳定放大器虽能实现低失调和抑制 flicker 噪声，但其开关电容输入网络导致输入阻抗（Zin）较低，且与斩波频率（Fch）和输入电容（Ci）的乘积成反比。当传感器输出阻抗（Zo）较高时，低输入阻抗会严重降低传感器灵敏度，限制了其在干电极ECG等高阻抗传感器接口中的应用。

Method: 提出差分电容翻转（Differential Capacitor Flipping）技术：在斩波放大器的输入级，通过在每个时钟周期重新配置输入电容的位置，使得电容两端的电压极性翻转而非充放电，从而在保持斩波操作的前提下，消除了开关电容网络产生的等效电阻，使输入阻抗理论上变为纯电容性且与斩波频率Fch无关。

Result: 采用TSMC 65 nm CMOS工艺实现，测得直流输入阻抗达21 GΩ；总功耗为2.6 μW（含时钟生成电路为2.8 μW）；1 Hz–150 Hz频带内总积分输入参考噪声为7.2 μVrms。

Conclusion: 该差分电容翻转技术有效打破了传统斩波放大器输入阻抗与斩波频率之间的折中关系，实现了极高的输入阻抗，适用于干电极ECG等需要高传感器输出阻抗的场景，在低功耗医疗级信号采集应用中具有显著优势。

Original Abstract: Chopper stabilized amplifiers are popularly used for realizing amplifiers with low offset and for rejecting flicker noise. One of the main limitations of these amplifiers is the low Input Impedance (Zin) produced by the switch capacitor input network. Zin here is resistive due to the switch capacitor action and is inversely proportional to the product of Chopping frequency (Fch) and Input Capacitance (Ci). Since Fch should be greater than the flicker noise corner frequency, this results in a low Zin. When interfacing sensors with high Sensor Output Impedance (Zo), chopper stabilized amplifiers load the sensors resulting in reduced sensitivity. This paper presents a novel input impedance boosting technique - Differential capacitor flipping technique for chopper based Capacitively Coupled Instrumentation Amplifier (CCIA), which prevents discharge and recharge of Ci's in every cycle by reconfiguring the capacitor positions while preserving the chopping operation. This ideally results in a purely capacitive Zin which is independent of Fch. The proposed architecture is used to demonstrate Electrocardiogram (ECG) signal acquisition with dry electrodes that have Zo in the order of a few Mega Ohms. This circuit implemented in TSMC 65 nm CMOS technology node features Zin of 21 GOhms at DC. The circuit has a power consumption of 2.6E(-6)W (2.8E(-6)W including clock generation circuits), with 7.2E(-6)Vrms (1 Hz-150 Hz) of total integrated input referred noise. ~

中文摘要翻译: 斩波稳定放大器广泛应用于实现低失调电压和抑制闪烁噪声的放大器设计。这类放大器的主要局限之一在于开关电容输入网络产生的低输入阻抗（Zin）。由于开关电容的作用，该Zin呈阻性，且与斩波频率（Fch）和输入电容（Ci）的乘积成反比。由于Fch必须高于闪烁噪声的转角频率，导致Zin较低。当与具有高传感器输出阻抗（Zo）的传感器接口时，斩波稳定放大器会加载传感器，从而降低灵敏度。本文提出一种新颖的输入阻抗提升技术——差分电容翻转技术，用于基于斩波的电容耦合仪表放大器（CCIA）。该技术通过在每个周期内重新配置电容位置，在保持斩波操作的同时避免了Ci的充放电。这使得Zin在理想情况下变为纯电容性，且与Fch无关。所提出的架构被用于演示使用输出阻抗为数兆欧的干电极进行心电图（ECG）信号采集。该电路采用TSMC 65 nm CMOS工艺实现，在直流下具有21 GΩ的输入阻抗。电路功耗为2.6E(-6)W（含时钟生成电路为2.8E(-6)W），总积分输入参考噪声为7.2E(-6)Vrms（1 Hz–150 Hz）。

</details>


### [91] [The Influence of Gain and Phase Mismatches on Beam Patterns in Phased Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13378)
*Jérémy Guichemerre,Christoph Studer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种频域框架，通过将相控阵波束方向图建模为锥削窗基函数在阵列变形上的映射，揭示了非理想增益、相位和延迟失配导致方向图副瓣抬升的频谱机理。基于此推导了随机失配下最大副瓣电平（SLL）的近似分布，实现了无需蒙特卡洛仿真的良率导向设计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究要么分析特定结构化失配模式，要么推导随机失配下的单角度边际统计量，均无法刻画最大副瓣电平（SLL）这类全局波束方向图指标，缺乏一种能系统分析失配影响并支持良率导向设计的理论框架。

Method: 提出频域框架：将方向图描述为锥削窗依赖的基函数沿阵列架构与信号带宽决定的变形上的取值；揭示元件误差产生理想方向图的加权副本，复本幅度由失配序列的离散傅里叶变换给出；基于此推导随机增益和相位失配下最大SLL分布的近似表达式。

Result: 推导了随机增益和相位失配下最大SLL分布的近似表达式，使得无需蒙特卡洛仿真即可快速评估设计良率与探索设计空间。

Conclusion: 该频域框架与解析表达式能够有效支持相控阵的良率导向设计和快速设计空间探索，避免了计算密集的蒙特卡洛仿真，弥补了现有方法无法评估全局波束方向图指标的不足。

Original Abstract: Practical implementations of phased arrays suffer from per-antenna gain, phase, and delay mismatches, which can significantly worsen the maximum sidelobe level (SLL) of beampatterns. The existing literature either analyzes specific structured mismatch patterns or derives per-angle marginal statistics under random mismatches, which fail to characterize global beampattern metrics such as the maximum SLL. To address this limitation, we propose a frequency-domain framework in which the beampattern is described by a tapering-window-dependent base function evaluated along a deformation determined by the array architecture and signal bandwidth. This formulation enables a spectral analysis of mismatches, revealing that element-wise errors generate weighted replicas of the ideal beampattern whose amplitudes are given by the discrete Fourier transform of the mismatch sequence. Building on this insight, we derive an approximation of the maximum SLL distribution under random gain and phase mismatches. The resulting expressions enable yield-oriented design and rapid design-space exploration without relying on computationally intensive Monte-Carlo simulations.

中文摘要翻译: 相控阵的实际部署中存在每根天线的增益、相位和时延失配，这些失配会显著恶化波束方向图的最大副瓣电平（SLL）。现有文献要么分析特定的结构化失配模式，要么在随机失配下推导单角度的边际统计量，这些方法均无法刻画最大副瓣电平（SLL）等全局波束方向图指标。为解决这一局限，我们提出一种频域框架，在该框架中，波束方向图被描述为一个依赖于锥削窗的基函数沿由阵列架构和信号带宽决定的变形上的取值。这一表述使得对失配进行频谱分析成为可能，揭示了单元级误差会产生理想方向图的加权副本，其幅值由失配序列的离散傅里叶变换给出。基于这一洞见，我们推导了随机增益和相位失配下最大副瓣电平（SLL）分布的近似表达式。所得结果使得良率导向设计和快速设计空间探索成为可能，而无需依赖计算密集的蒙特卡洛仿真。

</details>


### [92] [Towards Standardizing Affine Frequency Division Multiplexing (AFDM) for Future Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13416)
*Qu Luo,Lixia Xiao,Pei Xiao,Zilong Liu,Yin Xu,Qihao Peng,Zeping Sui,Hee Wook Kim,Hüseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文从标准化视角系统研究了仿射频分复用（AFDM）波形技术，论证了其与4G/5G多参数集框架、FMCW雷达波形及LoRa调制的后向兼容性，分析了多天线与多用户支持、峰均功率比等关键能力，并探讨了其在非地面网络、通感一体化、车联网和水声通信等新兴场景中的应用潜力，表明AFDM是未来无线网络的一种适时且有竞争力的技术。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: AFDM作为未来无线网络的一种有前景的波形候选，具有较强的抗双重选择性信道能力并能实现通信与感知功能的 seamless 融合，但缺乏从标准化视角对其进行的系统性研究。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用系统性分析方法，从标准化视角出发，首先介绍AFDM原理和波形标准化关键考量；然后检验AFDM与4G/5G多参数集框架、FMCW雷达波形和LoRa调制的后向兼容性；接着讨论多天线与多用户支持、PAPR等标准化关键能力；最后探讨AFDM在NTN、ISAC、V2X和UWA等新兴场景中的应用潜力。

Result: AFDM可以以有限的修改被纳入传统处理链中；在多重极端时延-多普勒色散场景下具备优异的波形鲁棒性；能够支持多天线和多用户系统；在标准化关键指标上表现良好。

Conclusion: AFDM是一种适时且具有竞争力的波形技术，适合用于未来无线网络。

Original Abstract: Affine frequency division multiplexing~(AFDM) has emerged as a compelling waveform candidate for future wireless networks, owing to its strong resilience to doubly selective channels and its ability to enable the seamless integration of communication and sensing functionalities. Against this context, this article provides a systematic study of AFDM from a standardization perspective. We first introduce the principles of AFDM and discuss the major considerations involved in waveform standardization. We then examine the backwards compatibility of AFDM with 4G/5G multi-numerology frameworks and their anticipated evolution, frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radar waveforms, and long-range (LoRa) modulation, demonstrating that AFDM can be incorporated into legacy processing chains with limited modification. Key standardization-critical capabilities are further discussed, including multiple-antenna and multi-user support, and peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR). Finally, we investigate the potential of AFDM in several emerging scenarios, including non-terrestrial networks~(NTN), integrated sensing and communications (ISAC), vehicle-to-everything (V2X), and underwater acoustic (UWA) communications, whereby severe delay-Doppler dispersion places stringent demands on waveform robustness. Through these explorations, it is shown that that AFDM represents a timely and compelling technology for future wireless networks.

中文摘要翻译: 仿射频分复用（AFDM）因其对双重选择性信道的强鲁棒性以及能够实现通信与感知功能的 seamless 融合，已成为未来无线网络的一种有吸引力的波形候选。在此背景下，本文从标准化视角对AFDM进行了系统性研究。我们首先介绍了AFDM的原理并讨论了波形标准化中涉及的主要考量。然后检验了AFDM与4G/5G多参数集框架及其预期演进、调频连续波（FMCW）雷达波形以及远距离（LoRa）调制的后向兼容性，证明AFDM可以以有限的修改被纳入传统处理链中。进一步讨论了若干标准化关键能力，包括多天线与多用户支持以及峰均功率比（PAPR）。最后，我们研究了AFDM在多个新兴场景中的潜力，包括非地面网络（NTN）、通感一体化（ISAC）、车联网（V2X）和水声通信（UWA），这些场景中严重的时延-多普勒色散对波形鲁棒性提出了严格要求。通过这些探索，结果表明AFDM是未来无线网络的一种适时且富有竞争力的技术。

</details>


### [93] [Mitigating SAR-ADC Non-Idealities in Massive MU-MIMO Systems via Affine Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13417)
*Jérémy Guichemerre,Christoph Studer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对实际SAR-ADC的非理想特性，提出了两种仿射模型（基于Bussgang分解和最大化SDR），并将其用于大规模MU-MIMO系统中，以低复杂度方法减轻ADC非理想性带来的性能损失。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文献几乎都采用理想化量化模型，忽略了真实ADC实现中固有的非理想特性，这限制了低分辨率数据转换器在实际大规模MU-MIMO系统中的性能评估与优化。

Method: 提出了两种仿射模型：一种基于Bussgang分解，另一种通过最大化信噪失真比（SDR）构建；并基于这些模型设计了低复杂度方法来减轻SAR-ADC非理想性对大规模MU-MIMO系统的影响。

Result: 所提出的两种仿射模型能够有效捕捉SAR-ADC的主要非理想特性，基于这些模型的低复杂度方法可以显著缓解非理想性对系统性能的负面影响。

Conclusion: 通过引入更真实的ADC非理想性模型，可以在低分辨率数据转换器的大规模MU-MIMO系统中实现更准确的性能评估和有效的补偿，从而兼顾低功耗、小面积与系统性能。

Original Abstract: Low-resolution data converters can significantly reduce the power consumption and silicon area of all-digital massive multi-user (MU) multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) basestations. However, the existing literature almost exclusively focuses on idealistic quantization models, neglecting the inherent non-idealities present in real-world analog-to-digital converter (ADC) implementations. To overcome this limitation, we propose two affine models, one based on Bussgang's decomposition and one that maximizes the signal-to-distortion ratio (SDR), both accounting for the most prominent non-idealities in successive approximation register (SAR) ADCs. Subsequently, we utilize these models to devise low-complexity methods that mitigate SAR-ADC non-idealities in massive MU-MIMO wireless systems.

中文摘要翻译: 低分辨率数据转换器可以显著降低全数字大规模多用户（MU）多输入多输出（MIMO）基站的功耗和硅片面积。然而，现有文献几乎都专注于理想化量化模型，忽略了实际模数转换器（ADC）实现中固有的非理想特性。为克服这一局限，我们提出了两种仿射模型，一种基于Bussgang分解，另一种通过最大化信噪失真比（SDR）构建，两者均考虑了逐次逼近寄存器（SAR）ADC中最突出的非理想特性。随后，我们利用这些模型设计了低复杂度方法，以减轻大规模MU-MIMO无线系统中SAR-ADC非理想特性的影响。

</details>


### [94] [Max-Min Secrecy Rate Optimization for Secure ISAC Networks: Global Optimization and Low-Complexity Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13582)
*Thanh-Nha To,Trung Quang Pham,Dang Y Hoang,Hoang-Lai Pham,Tuan Anh Pham*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究存在不可信感知用户窃听下的安全通感一体化系统，通过优化波束成形设计最大化用户间最小保密率，提出了基于分支定界（BB）的全局最优算法和基于逐次凸近似（SCA）的低复杂度算法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在通感一体化系统中，感知用户可能窃听通信用户的机密信息，现有工作缺乏对多用户场景下安全公平性的考虑，因此需要设计兼顾功率预算、感知质量和安全公平的波束成形方案。

Method: 1) 利用半定松弛（SDR）重构原非凸问题；2) 提出基于分支定界（BB）框架与凸松弛结合的全局最优求解算法；3) 提出基于逐次凸近似（SCA）的低复杂度迭代算法，通过求解一系列凸子问题逼近局部最优解。

Result: BB算法能够达到全局最优解，可作为性能评估基准；SCA算法在显著降低计算复杂度的同时能够获得接近最优的保密性能，适用于实际部署。

Conclusion: 本文提出的BB和SCA两种算法有效解决了安全ISAC系统中的非凸优化问题，SCA在复杂度与性能之间取得了良好折中，具有实际应用价值。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a secure integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) system in which multiple communication users (CUs) coexist with multiple untrusted sensing users (SUs) that may eavesdrop on the confidential information intended for the CUs. To promote security fairness among users, we formulate a max-min secrecy rate optimization problem subject to a transmit power budget and sensing quality requirements characterized by beampattern matching error constraints. The resulting design problem is highly non-convex due to the secrecy rate expressions and non-convex sensing constraints. To address these challenges, we first reformulate the problem using semidefinite relaxation (SDR). Based on the reformulated problem, we develop a branch-and-bound (BB) framework combined with convex relaxations to obtain the globally optimal solution within a prescribed accuracy. To further reduce computational complexity, we propose a low-complexity algorithm based on successive convex approximation (SCA), which iteratively solves a sequence of convex subproblems and converges to a local solution. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed BB algorithm achieves the global optimum and provides a benchmark for performance evaluation. Moreover, the proposed SCA-based algorithm attains near-optimal secrecy performance with significantly lower computational complexity, making it attractive for practical ISAC deployments.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了一种安全通感一体化系统，其中多个通信用户与多个不可信的感知用户共存，这些感知用户可能窃听旨在传输给通信用户的机密信息。为了促进用户间的安全公平性，我们构建了一个最大-最小保密率优化问题，受限于发射功率预算和由波束方向图匹配误差约束表征的感知质量要求。由于保密率表达式和非凸感知约束，所得设计问题高度非凸。为应对这些挑战，我们首先利用半定松弛对问题进行重构。基于重构后的问题，我们开发了一个结合凸松弛的分支定界框架，以在给定精度内获得全局最优解。为了进一步降低计算复杂度，我们提出了一种基于逐次凸近似的低复杂度算法，该算法迭代求解一系列凸子问题并收敛到局部解。数值结果表明，所提出的BB算法达到了全局最优，为性能评估提供了基准。此外，基于SCA的算法以显著更低的计算复杂度实现了接近最优的保密性能，使其适用于实际的通感一体化部署。

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Fed-FBD: Federated Functional Block Diversification for Isolation, Privacy, and Surgical Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12679)
*Weijie Chen,Alan B. McMillan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fed-FBD是一种模块化联邦学习架构，通过将ResNet分解为六个功能块并维护贡献者标记的块仓库，实现了块级隔离、隐私保护和高效机器遗忘，在仅牺牲0.3%-3.1% IID精度的代价下，提供了FedAvg所不具备的安全性保障。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准联邦学习（如FedAvg）将每个客户端视为黑箱，缺乏对恶意贡献者的隔离机制、无法审计单个客户端的影响、也无法满足已退出参与者的被遗忘权，因此需要一种更安全的联邦学习架构。

Method: 将ResNet骨干网络分解为六个功能块（stem、四个残差组和分类头），并维护一个包含N种颜色变体的仓库，每种颜色由独立追踪且带有贡献者戳记的功能块组装而成。Fed-FBD实现了块级隔离、基于设计的隐私保护以及亚秒级的手术式机器遗忘。

Result: 在MedMNIST-2D、PathMNIST (224x224) 和 CIFAR-10上的实验表明：Fed-FBD在充分大小数据集上仅牺牲0.3%-3.1%的IID准确率；在Dirichlet alpha=1.0下与FedAvg差距在0.8%-4.0%以内；所有六种对抗攻击都被限制在中毒客户端自身的块内，干净颜色上AUC漂移不超过±0.01。

Conclusion: Fed-FBD通过模块化分解和功能块仓库机制，以较小的精度代价实现了联邦学习中的块级隔离、隐私保护和高效遗忘，为联邦学习尤其是在医疗领域的安全部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw patient data, but standard approaches such as FedAvg treat each client as a black box and provide no mechanism for isolating an adversarial contributor, auditing per-client influence, or honoring a departed participant's right to be forgotten. We present Fed-FBD (Federated Functional Block Diversification), a modular federated architecture that decomposes a ResNet backbone into six functional blocks (the stem, four residual groups, and the classification head) and maintains a warehouse of N color variants, each assembled from independently tracked and contributor-stamped blocks. Fed-FBD provides three capabilities absent in FedAvg: (i) architecturally guaranteed block-level isolation, so that an adversarial or mislabelled client cannot contaminate the clean colous; (ii) privacy-by-design, where membership inference advantage is already indistinguishable from chance before any privacy mechanism is applied; and (iii) surgical machine unlearning of a departed participant's contribution at sub-second cost and without retraining. Experiments on six MedMNIST-2D datasets, PathMNIST at 224x224, and CIFAR-10 show that Fed-FBD trades a modest 0.3%-3.1% IID accuracy gap on the adequately sized datasets for these guarantees, remains within 0.8%-4.0% of FedAvg at Dirichlet alpha=1.0 on three of four datasets, and confines all six adversarial attacks we study to the poisoned client's own blocks with at most +/-0.01 AUC drift on the clean colors.

中文摘要翻译: 联邦学习（FL）能够在无需共享原始患者数据的前提下实现协作模型训练，但FedAvg等标准方法将每个客户端视为黑箱，无法隔离恶意贡献者、审计单个客户端的影响，也无法满足已退出参与者的被遗忘权。我们提出Fed-FBD（联邦功能块多样化），这是一种模块化联邦架构，它将ResNet骨干网络分解为六个功能块（stem、四个残差组和分类头），并维护一个包含N种颜色变体的仓库，每种颜色由独立追踪且带有贡献者戳记的功能块组装而成。Fed-FBD提供了FedAvg所不具备的三项能力：(i) 架构保障的块级隔离，使得恶意或错误标注的客户端无法污染干净的颜色变体；(ii) 设计层面的隐私保护，在应用任何隐私机制之前，成员推断优势已与随机猜测无区别；(iii) 手术式机器遗忘，可在亚秒级成本内且无需重训练即可移除已退出参与者的贡献。在六个MedMNIST-2D数据集、224x224分辨率的PathMNIST以及CIFAR-10上的实验表明，Fed-FBD在充分大小的数据集上以0.3%-3.1%的IID准确率差距换取这些保障，在Dirichlet alpha=1.0的设置下，四个数据集中有三个与FedAvg的差距保持在0.8%-4.0%以内，并且我们研究的所有六种对抗攻击均被限制在中毒客户端自身的块内，干净颜色上的AUC漂移不超过±0.01。

</details>


### [96] [Quickest Detection of Hallucination Onset: Delay Bounds and Learned CUSUM Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12476)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了将词级幻觉检测重新定义为"最快变化检测"问题，并利用CUSUM框架和序贯分析揭示了传统AUC评估无法捕捉的检测延迟结构，发现当前方法的检测延迟理论上界约为1.3个token，而实际方法延迟在11-13个token，差距源于学习到的得分仅捕获了特征中1/4.5的散度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统词级幻觉检测器使用AUC等分类指标评估所有token，但流式监控器更关注从幻觉发生到报警的延迟时间。因此需要将幻觉检测重新建模为最快变化检测问题，以更好地反映实际部署场景。

Method: 将幻觉检测形式化为最快变化检测问题；基于RAGTruth数据验证了潜在忠实/幻觉状态的一阶马尔可夫模型；引入Lorden下界刻画理论最优检测延迟；使用因果循环标注器作为CUSUM（累积和）检测器，学习增量函数；通过Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优性定理分析差距来源。

Result: 在虚警率0.01下，Lorden下界显示理论最优延迟约1.3个token；因果循环标注器（CUSUM）检测延迟为11-13个token，优于线性逐token基线（31个token）；性能优势主要归因于更好的逐token得分而非时间累积；学习到的得分仅实现特征中散度的1/4.5，该差距无法通过重新校准消除，剩余部分由有限时域效应解释。

Conclusion: 分类指标（如AUC）掩盖了检测延迟结构，序贯分析使延迟可测量；当前方法的检测延迟与理论下界之间存在数量级差距，主要源于学习得分的信息提取不足。

Original Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

中文摘要翻译: 词级幻觉检测器通常作为分类器进行评估，即计算所有token上的AUC。然而，流式监控器的评价标准是其反应时间：从幻觉发生到报警之间经过的token数量。本文将幻觉起始检测形式化为最快变化检测问题。基于RAGTruth验证的潜在忠实/幻觉状态的一阶马尔可夫模型，将任务置于经典变点理论框架内，并得出Lorden检测延迟下界：在虚警率为0.01时约为1.3个token。我们进一步证明，因果循环标注器实际上是一种带有学习增量的CUSUM检测器；在匹配的虚警率下，其检测延迟为11-13个token，而线性逐token基线为31个token。受控分解表明，该优势主要归因于更好的逐token得分，而非时间累积。Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优性定理解释了剩余的数量级差距：学习到的得分仅实现了特征所携带散度的1/4.5，这一缺陷无法通过重新校准消除，剩余部分为有限时域效应。分类指标掩盖了这种延迟结构，而序贯分析使其变得可测量。

</details>


### [97] [Boltzmann Attention: Learnable Ising Couplings for Cooperative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12478)
*Gilhan Kim,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Boltzmann注意力机制，通过可学习的Ising模型耦合项引入位置间显式交互，优于标准softmax注意力，且可利用量子退火进行训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准注意力机制仅通过查询-键相似度计算相关性，软最大化归一化虽引入位置间竞争，但未显式参数化注意力决策之间的可学习交互，难以直接建模注意力内部的合作或对抗性共注意力结构。

Method: 提出Boltzmann注意力，基于能量模型泛化注意力机制，用可交互的Ising模型控制注意力模式。在常规数据相关局域场基础上增加可学习的成对耦合项，使模型能够表示超出softmax或sigmoid注意力的位置间相关性。

Result: 在字符级语言建模和合成括号匹配实验中，Boltzmann注意力在标准Transformer架构下持续优于softmax注意力，且优势随序列长度增加而加大。四路消融实验证实改进来源于可学习的成对耦合项。此外，使用绝热量子退火作为实际训练方法可与精确Boltzmann计算保持竞争性能。

Conclusion: 显式的位置间交互为基于注意力的序列建模提供了原则性增强，Ising公式化为基于量子计算的采样策略开辟了自然路径。

Original Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern sequence models, yet standard attention computes relevance primarily through individual query--key similarities. Although softmax normalization introduces competition among positions, a standard attention layer does not explicitly parameterize learnable interactions between attention decisions. This limits its ability to directly model cooperative or antagonistic co-attention structure within the attention mechanism itself. We propose Boltzmann attention, an energy-based generalization in which attention patterns are governed by an interacting Ising model. The method augments the usual data-dependent local fields with learnable pairwise couplings, allowing the model to represent inter-position correlations beyond those captured by softmax or sigmoid attention. Experiments on character-level language modeling and synthetic bracket matching show that Boltzmann attention consistently improves over standard softmax attention within a standard Transformer architecture, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as sequence length increases. A four-way ablation confirms that the improvement arises from the learnable pairwise couplings. These results suggest that explicit inter-position interactions provide a principled enhancement for attention-based sequence modeling. Moreover, the Ising formulation opens a natural path toward quantum-computing-based sampling strategies: we demonstrate that diabatic quantum annealing provides a practical training method while maintaining competitive performance with exact Boltzmann computation.

中文摘要翻译: 注意力机制是现代序列模型的核心，然而标准注意力主要通过单个查询-键相似度计算相关性。尽管软最大化归一化引入了位置间的竞争，但标准注意力层并未显式参数化注意力决策之间的可学习交互。这限制了其在注意力机制内部直接建模合作性或对抗性共注意力结构的能力。本文提出Boltzmann注意力，一种基于能量的泛化方法，其中注意力模式由可交互的Ising模型控制。该方法在常规数据相关的局域场基础上增加了可学习的成对耦合项，使模型能够表示超出softmax或sigmoid注意力所捕获的位置间相关性。在字符级语言建模和合成括号匹配实验上，Boltzmann注意力在标准Transformer架构下持续优于标准softmax注意力，且优势随序列长度增加而更加显著。四路消融实验证实，性能提升源于可学习的成对耦合项。这些结果表明，显式的位置间交互为基于注意力的序列建模提供了一种原则性增强。此外，Ising公式化为基于量子计算的采样策略开辟了自然路径：我们证明绝热量子退火提供了一种实用的训练方法，同时保持与精确Boltzmann计算相竞争的性能。

</details>


### [98] [ReCal: Reward Calibration for RL-based LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12479)
*Qihang Yu,Hanwen Tong,Zhengqi Zhang,Bo Zheng,Feng Wei,Shengyu Zhang,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReCal框架，通过层次化奖励分解与分布感知优化策略，解决RL路由中多目标奖励冲突与样本难度异质导致的优化偏差问题，在多数据集上提升路由性能与训练稳定性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的LLM路由方法在处理异构任务时，面临两个关键问题：一是多目标（如正确性、格式规范）被聚合为单标量奖励，导致信用分配模糊与优化信号冲突；二是不同实例的奖励信号方差差异大，引入偏向简单样本的优化偏差。现有方法无法提供信息量充足且可比较的学习信号。

Method: 提出ReCal框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 层次化奖励分解机制，将奖励分解到各目标维度并计算分量级优势估计；(2) 分布感知优化策略，通过方差感知重加权和每数据集归一化来校准优化过程中的变异性。

Result: 在7个数据集上的实验表明，ReCal在路由性能和训练稳定性上一致优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过奖励分解与分布感知校准，能有效缓解RL路由中的多目标冲突与样本异质性偏差，提升路由质量与训练稳定性。

Original Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as an effective paradigm for leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple LLMs through dynamic model and reasoning-strategy selection. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based routing methods further improve routing quality by optimizing routing policies from interaction feedback. However, they still struggle to provide informative and comparable learning signals under heterogeneous tasks with varying difficulty. In practice, multiple objectives (e.g., correctness, format behavior) are aggregated into a single scalar reward, leading to ambiguous credit assignment and conflicting optimization signals. Moreover, reward signals exhibit significant variability across instances, where some instances produce higher or more variable rewards, introducing optimization bias that favors trivial samples over informative ones. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{ReCal}, a \textbf{\underline{Re}}ward \textbf{\underline{Cal}}ibration framework for RL-based LLM routing. We first introduce a hierarchical reward decomposition mechanism with component-wise advantage estimation. We further propose a distribution-aware optimization strategy that calibrates optimization variability through variance-aware reweighting and per-dataset normalization. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that ReCal consistently improves routing performance, and training stability over baselines. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLM）路由已成为一种有效范式，通过动态的模型与推理策略选择来利用多个LLM的互补优势。最近的基于强化学习（RL）的路由方法通过从交互反馈中优化路由策略，进一步提升了路由质量。然而，它们仍然难以在难度各异的异构任务下提供具有信息量且可比较的学习信号。在实践中，多个目标（如正确性、格式行为）被聚合为单一标量奖励，导致模糊的信用分配和冲突的优化信号。此外，奖励信号在不同实例间表现出显著变异性——某些实例产生更高或更易变的奖励——这引入了偏向简单样本而非信息性样本的优化偏差。为解决这些问题，我们提出了 ReCal，一个面向基于RL的LLM路由的奖励校准框架。我们首先引入了一种带有分量级优势估计的层次化奖励分解机制。进一步，我们提出了一种分布感知优化策略，通过方差感知重加权和每数据集归一化来校准优化变异性。在七个数据集上的实验表明，ReCal 在路由性能和训练稳定性上始终优于基线方法。代码已开源：https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal。

</details>


### [99] [Representing Time Series as Structured Programs for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12481)
*Jaeho Kim,Changhun Oh,Seokhyun Lee,Irina Rish,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出T2SP方法，将时间序列转化为结构化符号程序表示，使预训练LLM无需微调即可高效推理时间序列，在编辑、描述和问答三项任务上优于原始序列表示。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将原始数值序列直接输入LLM或微调预训练模型，不仅导致模态不匹配、长序列性能下降，还带来大量计算开销。亟需一种无需训练、对齐LLM原生模态的时间序列表示方法。

Method: T2SP（Time-Series-to-Structured-Program）是一种确定性的、无需训练的方法，将时间序列分解为趋势、周期和显著事件，并以结构化的符号程序格式表达，使其对齐LLM预训练所基于的文本和代码模态。

Result: 在编辑、描述和问答三个推理任务上，T2SP相比原始字符串表示一致提升了性能，缩短了推理时间，并降低了任务失败率。

Conclusion: T2SP为时间序列与LLM之间提供了一种有效的接口，通过将时序结构提取从模型转移到表示本身，使得现成的LLM能够直接利用其已有的推理能力进行时间序列理解。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning and instruction-following capabilities, making them potentially powerful tools for time-series analysis. However, time series lie outside their native textual modality, raising a fundamental question: how should time series be represented so that LLMs can reason about them effectively? Existing work typically serializes raw numerical sequences or fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs on time-series data. These approaches place the burden of extracting temporal structure directly on the LLM, creating a modality mismatch that often degrades performance on long sequences and introduces substantial computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Time-Series-to-Structured-Program representation (T2SP), a deterministic, training-free method that represents a time series as a structured symbolic program. T2SP decomposes time series into trends, periods, and salient events, expressing them in a program-friendly format aligned with the textual and code-like modalities on which LLMs are natively trained. By shifting temporal-structure extraction from the model to the representation itself, T2SP enables off-the-shelf LLMs to leverage their existing reasoning capabilities for time-series understanding. We evaluate T2SP on three reasoning tasks -- editing, captioning, and question answering -- where it consistently improves performance, reduces reasoning time, and lowers failure rates compared with raw-string representations. Our results demonstrate that T2SP provides an effective interface between time series and LLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLM）展现出强大的推理和指令遵循能力，使其成为时间序列分析的潜在强大工具。然而，时间序列处于其原生文本模态之外，这引发了一个基本问题：应如何表示时间序列，才能使LLM对其进行有效推理？现有工作通常将原始数值序列序列化，或在时间序列数据上微调预训练的LLM。这些方法将提取时序结构的负担直接压在LLM上，造成了模态不匹配，常常导致长序列性能下降并引入大量计算开销。本文提出时间序列到结构化程序表示（T2SP），一种确定性的、无需训练的方法，将时间序列表示为一个结构化的符号程序。T2SP将时间序列分解为趋势、周期和显著事件，并以与LLM原生训练的文本和代码模态对齐的、适合程序处理的格式进行表达。通过将时序结构提取从模型转移到表示本身，T2SP使得现成的LLM能够利用其已有的推理能力进行时间序列理解。我们在三个推理任务——编辑、描述和问答——上评估T2SP，与原始字符串表示相比，T2SP一致地提升了性能，缩短了推理时间，并降低了失败率。我们的结果表明，T2SP为时间序列与LLM之间提供了一种有效的接口。

</details>


### [100] [Extracting Governing Equations from Latent Dynamics via Multi-View Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13260)
*Paolo Muratore,Mackenzie Weygandt Mathis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DYSCO是一种多视角时序对比学习算法，能从带噪声的高维观测中联合恢复潜在轨迹与动力学方程，并在理论上保证仿射不确定性下的强可识别性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从带噪声的高维观测数据中识别潜在动力系统是表示学习、系统辨识和科学发现的交叉核心问题，现有方法在噪声非线性观测场景下的可识别性保证不足。

Method: 提出DYSCO，一种多视角时序对比学习算法，利用同一底层过程的多个独立噪声视角来分离信号与噪声；将动力学参数化于结构化函数基中，实现仿射规范下控制方程的符号恢复。

Result: 在混沌、振荡和亚稳态等多种动力学机制下，在Gaussian和Poisson观测噪声下均准确恢复了潜在轨迹和流场；Poisson噪声场景尤其适用于神经记录。

Conclusion: DYSCO在理论上将先前的可识别性结果扩展到噪声非线性观测的现实场景，实验上在多种动力学场景中均实现了准确的潜变量与动力学恢复。

Original Abstract: Identifying latent dynamical systems from noisy, high-dimensional measurements is a central problem at the intersection of representation learning, system identification, and scientific discovery. We present DYSCO, a multi-view temporal contrastive learning algorithm that jointly recovers latent trajectories and the governing dynamics from such observations, by leveraging multiple independent noisy views of the same underlying process to disentangle signal from noise. By parameterizing the dynamics in a structured functional basis, our framework further enables symbolic recovery of the governing equations within an affine gauge. We offer theoretical guarantees for strong identification up to an affine indeterminacy, extending prior identifiability results to the realistic setting of noisy nonlinear observations. Empirically, we demonstrate accurate recovery of both latent trajectories and flow fields across a diverse set of dynamical regimes (e.g., chaotic, oscillatory, and metastable) under both Gaussian and Poisson observation noise, the latter being particularly relevant for neural recordings.

中文摘要翻译: 从带噪声的高维观测中识别潜在动力系统，是表示学习、系统辨识和科学发现交叉领域的一个核心问题。我们提出DYSCO，一种多视角时序对比学习算法，通过利用同一底层过程的多个独立噪声视角来分离信号与噪声，从而从这类观测中联合恢复潜在轨迹与支配动力学。通过将动力学参数化于结构化函数基中，我们的框架进一步实现了仿射规范下控制方程的符号恢复。我们提供了强可识别性（直至仿射不确定性）的理论保证，将先前的可识别性结果扩展到了噪声非线性观测的现实场景。在实验上，我们在多种动力学机制（如混沌、振荡和亚稳态）下，在Gaussian和Poisson观测噪声下均展示了潜在轨迹和流场的准确恢复，其中Poisson噪声场景对神经记录尤为重要。

</details>


### [101] [Scalable anomaly detection via a univariate Christoffel function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12483)
*Florian Grivet,Didier Henrion,Jean-Bernard Lasserre,Louise Travé-Massuyès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），通过基于查询点与支持点之间平方距离的简化计算，解决了Christoffel函数在高维数据中因矩阵求逆导致的计算瓶颈，在异常检测基准ADBench上优于14个基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Christoffel函数方法虽具有坚实的数学基础和低计算开销的优势，但因需要逆矩阵且矩阵规模随数据维度指数增长，导致其在中高维数据集上难以实际应用。本文旨在克服这一维度限制，同时保留该方法关键的理论性质。

Method: 提出UCF（Univariate Christoffel Function），基于查询点与支持点之间的平方距离来定义单变量形式的Christoffel函数，从而避免了对高维矩阵的求逆操作，大幅降低了计算复杂度。

Result: 在ADBench基准上的大量实验表明，UCF在平均精度（Average Precision）指标上一致优于14个最先进的基线方法，有效解决了Christoffel函数的可扩展性瓶颈。

Conclusion: 通过解决Christoffel函数的可扩展性瓶颈，本文为异常检测工具箱贡献了一种鲁棒、有理论依据且普遍适用的方法。

Original Abstract: Anomaly detection plays a critical role in identifying unusual patterns across domains such as fraud detection, network intrusion, and system fault diagnosis. Recently, Christoffel function-based methods, rooted in polynomial optimization, have emerged as promising alternatives to deep learning due to their strong mathematical foundations and computational frugality. However, their practical applicability is hindered by the need to invert a matrix whose size grows exponentially with the data dimension, rendering the method intractable even for moderate-dimensional datasets. This paper addresses the dimensionality limitations of Christoffel function-based anomaly detection while preserving its key theoretical properties, i.e., the on-off support dichotomy behavior and the accurate support shape capture. We introduce UCF, a univariate Christoffel function which is based on the squared distance between the query point and the support points. Extensive experiments on the ADBench benchmark demonstrate that UCF consistently outperforms 14 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Average Precision. By resolving the scalability bottleneck of the Christoffel Function, this work expands the toolkit of anomaly detection methods with a robust, theoretically grounded, and universally applicable approach.

中文摘要翻译: 异常检测在欺诈检测、网络入侵和系统故障诊断等领域中发挥着识别异常模式的关键作用。近年来，基于Christoffel函数的方法（根植于多项式优化）因其坚实的数学基础和低计算开销，已成为深度学习的有前景的替代方案。然而，其实际应用受到需要求逆一个规模随数据维度指数增长的矩阵的限制，即使对于中等维度的数据集，该方法也变得不可行。本文解决了基于Christoffel函数的异常检测的维度局限性，同时保留了其关键的理论性质，即开-关支撑二象性行为和准确的支撑形状捕获能力。我们提出了UCF，一种基于查询点与支撑点之间平方距离的单变量Christoffel函数。在ADBench基准上的大量实验表明，UCF在平均精度指标上一致优于14个最先进的基线方法。通过解决Christoffel函数的可扩展性瓶颈，本文为异常检测方法工具箱贡献了一种鲁棒、有理论依据且普遍适用的方法。

</details>


### [102] [Speculative Rollback Correction for Quality-Diverse Web Agent Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12485)
*Longkun Hao,Hongyu Lin,Hao Li,Zhichao Yang,Haojie Hao,Dongshuo Huang,Haitao Yang,Hongyu Ge,Ming jie Xie,Yanjun Wu,Zi Hao Yin,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为推测性回滚修正(SRC)的分支级模仿学习框架，通过固定视界分支审查和回滚机制，在交互式Web代理训练中平衡早期错误累积与过度依赖专家策略的问题，在WebArena-Infinity上收集了977条通过验证器的轨迹和9,183个下一步动作示例。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在交互式Web代理的模仿学习中，专家干预的时机存在两难困境：延迟干预会导致早期错误累积使页面状态不可恢复；而过早或过度干预则会使代理过度依赖专家策略，陷入单一僵化轨迹的局部最优。

Method: 提出推测性回滚修正(SRC)框架：学生先执行一段固定视界的推测性短分支，然后由教师审查；教师仅在局部进展受阻时定位第一个有害偏差；通过回滚保留有益前缀；成功的轨迹由硬验证器过滤并存入轻量级质量-多样性存档；最终对局部修正和通过验证器的轨迹进行下一动作监督微调。

Result: 在WebArena-Infinity上，SRC收集了977条通过验证器的轨迹和9,183个下一步动作示例；固定视界审查相比逐步审查在恢复与查询的权衡上更优，同时保留了通过验证器的解决方案变体。

Conclusion: SRC通过分支级推测执行与回滚修正机制，有效解决了模仿学习中专家干预时机难以把握的问题，在数据效率和轨迹多样性之间取得了更好的平衡。

Original Abstract: Training interactive web agents through imitation learning from expert trajectories has emerged as a highly effective approach. However, determining the optimal timing for expert intervention presents a critical challenge in this context. Delayed intervention often leads to the accumulation of early-stage errors, pushing the page state into an irrecoverable regime. Conversely, premature or excessive intervention causes the agent to become overly reliant on expert policies, trapping the model in local optima characterized by a single, rigid trajectory. We propose Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC), a branch-level imitation framework for resettable agent environments. Instead of requesting teacher labels at every visited state or correcting only after a completed trajectory, SRC uses fixed-horizon branch review: the student executes a short speculative segment before teacher review, and the teacher localizes the first harmful deviation only when local progress breaks. Rollback preserves useful prefixes, while successful rollouts are filtered by a hard verifier and retained in a lightweight quality-diversity archive. The resulting data supports next-action supervised fine-tuning on both localized corrections and verifier-passing trajectories. On WebArena-Infinity, SRC collects 977 verifier-passing trajectories and 9,183 next-action examples; fixed-horizon review improves the recovery-versus-query tradeoff over step-level review while retaining verifier-passing solution variants. Code is available at https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent.

中文摘要翻译: 通过从专家轨迹中进行模仿学习来训练交互式Web代理已成为一种高效的方法。然而，确定专家干预的最佳时机在此背景下是一个关键挑战。延迟干预通常会导致早期错误累积，将页面状态推入不可恢复的境地。反之，过早或过度干预会导致代理过度依赖专家策略，使模型陷入以单一僵化轨迹为特征的局部最优。我们提出了推测性回滚修正(Speculative Rollback Correction, SRC)，一种适用于可重置代理环境的**分支级模仿框架**。不同于在每个访问状态都请求教师标签或仅在完整轨迹完成后才进行修正，SRC采用**固定视界分支审查**：学生先执行一个短的推测性片段，然后由教师审查；教师仅在局部进展受阻时才定位第一个有害偏差。**回滚**机制保留了有益前缀，而成功的展开轨迹则由**硬验证器**过滤并存入轻量级**质量-多样性存档**中。产生的数据支持在局部修正和通过验证器的轨迹上进行**下一动作监督微调**。在WebArena-Infinity上，SRC收集了977条通过验证器的轨迹和9,183个下一步动作示例；固定视界审查相比逐步审查在恢复与查询的权衡上更优，同时保留了通过验证器的解决方案变体。代码可在https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent获取。

</details>


### [103] [An Empirical Study on Predictive Maintenance for Component X in Heavy-Duty Scania Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12486)
*Valeriu Dimidov,Sasan Jafarnejad,Raphaël Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于状态预测性维护（PdM）方法，该方法假设被监测部件的磨损状态可表示为单调非递减时间序列，仅选取最近观测值并转换为表格格式进行分类，在Scania Component X数据集上降低了成本并简化了建模过程。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 卡车车队的状态预测性维护面临数据量大、故障检测复杂、解决方案成本效益难以权衡等挑战，现有方法难以在降低成本和简化实现之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 基于被监测部件磨损状态可表示为单调非递减时间序列的假设，仅从时间序列中选取最近观测值，将其转换为表格格式，然后使用适用于表格数据的机器学习模型（通过AutoML实现）进行分类。

Result: 在Scania Component X数据集上，所提方法相比现有最优（SOTA）方法降低了成本，同时通过AutoML简化了建模流程。

Conclusion: 提出的条件预测性维护方法在降低成本方面优于现有最优方法，且通过AutoML简化了建模过程，具有实际应用价值。

Original Abstract: Condition-based Predictive Maintenance (PdM) for truck fleets has gained momentum in recent years. This maintenance strategy aims to minimize unplanned downtimes and reduce costs by monitoring the health status of vehicles and taking proactive action based on their condition. However, the implementation of condition-based PdM systems is challenging due to the large volume of data generated by the trucks, the inherent complexity of detecting failures through sensor data and the difficulties in finding cost-effective trade-offs in the solution's implementation. In this paper, we define and validate a condition-based PdM methodology built on the assumption that the wear-and-tear state of the monitored component can be represented as a monotonically non-decreasing time series. It involves selecting only the most recent observations from the time series and transforming them into a tabular format for classification using machine learning (ML) models designed for tabular data. Our results indicate that the proposed methodology reduces costs on the Scania Component X dataset compared to current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, while also simplifying the modeling process through AutoML.

中文摘要翻译: 针对卡车车队的状态预测性维护（PdM）近年来发展迅速。该维护策略旨在通过监测车辆的健康状态并基于其状况采取主动措施，从而最小化非计划停机时间并降低成本。然而，由于卡车产生的大量数据、通过传感器数据检测故障的固有复杂性以及在解决方案实施中寻找成本效益平衡的困难，状态PdM系统的实现颇具挑战。本文定义并验证了一种基于状态的条件预测性维护方法，该方法建立在被监测部件的磨损状态可表示为单调非递减时间序列的假设之上。它仅从时间序列中选取最近的观测值，并将其转换为表格格式，以便使用适用于表格数据的机器学习（ML）模型进行分类。我们的结果表明，与当前最优（SOTA）方法相比，所提方法在Scania Component X数据集上降低了成本，同时通过AutoML简化了建模过程。

</details>


### [104] [DynamicPTQ: Mitigating Activation Quantization Collapse via Residual-Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12487)
*Zimo Zhao,Maolin Wang,Bowen Yu,Bowen Liu,Xiao Han,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DynamicPTQ，一种动态后训练量化策略，通过识别残差流中跨层动态导致的量化敏感层，仅对这些层分配8位激活精度，其余保持4位，在W4A4KV4量化下提升LLM推理性能与效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PTQ方法通过变换（如正交旋转、仿射缩放）缓解激活值中的巨大激活问题，但忽略了残差流的跨层动态——巨大激活以阶段方式出现和消失，导致残差剧烈变化，使静态变换无法解决动态量化不稳定性。

Method: 提出DynamicPTQ，通过引入Jump Ratio和Historical Feature SNR两个指标表征残差流跨层动态，识别量化敏感层，对这些层分配8位激活精度，其余权重、KV缓存和其他激活保持4位精度，可与QuaRot、SpinQuant、FlatQuant等强基线集成。

Result: 在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上的实验表明，DynamicPTQ在W4A4KV4量化下持续提升困惑度和零样本QA性能，同时实现1.05至1.07倍吞吐量提升，内存开销适中。

Conclusion: 动态的、面向阶段的混合精度激活量化是解决跨层残差动态导致的不稳定性的有效路径，为实现鲁棒的低比特LLM推理提供了实用方案。

Original Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for efficient large language model inference, but reliably quantizing activations remains challenging when weights, activations, and KV caches are all quantized to 4-bit precision. A key difficulty lies in massive activations, whose extreme values dominate the activation range and amplify quantization errors. State-of-the-art methods mainly mitigate massive activations through transformation-based smoothing, such as orthogonal rotations and affine scaling, but overlook the cross-layer dynamics of the residual stream. In this paper, we show that massive activations emerge and disappear in a phase-wise pattern across network depth, triggering large residual changes. These changes cause newly injected layer-wise updates to dominate the 4-bit quantization scale and weaken historical residual information. To characterize this behavior, we introduce Jump Ratio and Historical Feature SNR. This suggests that static transformation-based smoothing cannot fully resolve dynamic quantization instability caused by cross-layer residual changes. Based on this analysis, we propose DynamicPTQ, a Dynamic Post-Training Quantization policy for phase-aware mixed-precision activation quantization. DynamicPTQ identifies quantization-sensitive layers from residual-stream dynamics and assigns 8-bit activation precision only to these layers, while keeping weights, KV caches, and other activations in 4-bit precision. It can be directly integrated with strong PTQ baselines such as QuaRot, SpinQuant, and FlatQuant. Experiments on LLaMA-2 and LLaMA-3 show that DynamicPTQ consistently improves perplexity and zero-shot QA performance under W4A4KV4 quantization, while achieving 1.05 to 1.07 times throughput improvement with modest memory overhead. These results demonstrate a practical path toward robust low-bit LLM inference.

中文摘要翻译: 后训练量化（PTQ）对于高效的大语言模型推理至关重要，但当权重、激活值和KV缓存全部量化至4位精度时，可靠地量化激活值仍然具有挑战性。一个关键困难在于巨大激活值，其极端数值主导了激活范围并放大量化误差。最先进的方法主要通过基于变换的平滑技术（如正交旋转和仿射缩放）来缓解巨大激活问题，但忽略了残差流的跨层动态。本文表明，巨大激活值以阶段式的模式在网络深度上出现和消失，引发较大的残差变化。这些变化导致新注入的逐层更新主导4位量化尺度，并削弱历史残差信息。为表征这一行为，我们引入了跳跃比和歷史特征信噪比。这表明基于静态变换的平滑方法无法完全解决由跨层残差变化引起的动态量化不稳定性。基于此分析，我们提出了DynamicPTQ，一种面向阶段的混合精度激活量化的动态后训练量化策略。DynamicPTQ从残差流动中识别量化敏感层，仅对这些层分配8位激活精度，同时保持权重、KV缓存和其他激活为4位精度。它可以直接集成到QuaRot、SpinQuant和FlatQuant等强PTQ基线中。在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上的实验表明，DynamicPTQ在W4A4KV4量化下持续改善困惑度和零样本问答性能，同时实现1.05至1.07倍的吞吐量提升，且内存开销适中。这些结果展示了实现鲁棒低比特LLM推理的一条实用路径。

</details>


### [105] [A Stationary (and Therefore Compatible) Representation is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12488)
*Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Simone Ricci,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了d-Simplex固定分类器学习的平稳表示在形式定义上具有兼容性，并提出通过交叉熵损失与对比损失的凸组合来捕获高阶依赖关系，在模型持续更新与替换的场景下实现了无需重新处理图库图像的最优检索性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 学习兼容表示旨在使模型更新后新旧特征可互换使用，但现有d-Simplex固定分类器在交叉熵损失下仅对齐一阶统计量，无法充分捕获模型更新间表示中的高阶依赖关系。

Method: 采用d-Simplex固定分类器，通过交叉熵损失与对比损失的凸组合进行训练，该方式不仅能捕获高阶依赖，且在兼容性约束下等价于单独使用交叉熵损失进行学习。

Result: 在模型顺序微调和替换的新场景中，平稳表示支持无需重建图库图像的连续检索服务，同时提升模型更新和替换期间的性能，达到state-of-the-art水平。

Conclusion: 平稳表示能够实现无中断的检索服务，在模型持续更新场景下具有重要实用价值，为未来兼容表示学习研究奠定了理论基础。

Original Abstract: Learning compatible representations aims to learn feature representations that can be used interchangeably over time whenever a model undergoes updates. In this paper, we demonstrate that stationary representations learned by d-Simplex fixed classifiers imply compatibility as in its formal definition. This result establishes a foundation for future works and can be directly exploited in practical learning scenarios. We address the challenge of learning compatibility using $d$-Simplex fixed classifiers when the model is sequentially fine-tuned. Learning according to a d-Simplex fixed classifier with the cross-entropy loss aligns feature distributions at the first-order statistics. Consequently, it may not fully capture higher-order dependencies in the representation between model updates. To address this issue, we demonstrate that training the model using a $d$-Simplex fixed classifier through a convex combination of the cross-entropy loss and a contrastive loss not only captures higher-order dependencies, but is also equivalent to learning with the cross-entropy under the compatibility constraints. We confirm our findings with extensive experiments also considering a new scenario where a pre-trained model is sequentially fine-tuned and occasionally replaced with an improved model. We show that stationary representations enable uninterrupted retrieval services (without reprocessing gallery images) while improving performance during model updates and replacements, achieving state-of-the-art. Code at https://github.com/miccunifi/iamcl2r.

中文摘要翻译: 学习兼容表示旨在获得这样的特征表示：当模型随时间进行更新时，这些特征表示可以被互换使用。本文证明，由d-Simplex固定分类器学习到的平稳表示在形式定义上具有兼容性。这一结果为未来研究奠定了基础，并可直接应用于实际学习场景。我们解决了当模型被顺序微调时，使用d-Simplex固定分类器学习兼容性的挑战。基于交叉熵损失的d-Simplex固定分类器学习仅对齐了一阶统计量上的特征分布，因此可能无法充分捕获模型更新间表示中的高阶依赖关系。为解决这一问题，我们证明，使用d-Simplex固定分类器并通过交叉熵损失与对比损失的凸组合进行训练，不仅能捕获高阶依赖关系，而且在兼容性约束下等价于仅使用交叉熵损失进行学习。我们通过大量实验验证了上述发现，实验还考虑了一个新场景：预训练模型被顺序微调，并偶尔被更优模型替换。结果表明，平稳表示能够支持无需重新处理图库图像的无中断检索服务，同时在模型更新和替换期间提升性能，达到了最优水平。代码地址：https://github.com/miccunifi/iamcl2r。

</details>


### [106] [Robustness Verification of Recurrent Neural Networks with Abstraction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12490)
*Li-Jen Lin,Chih-Duo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种抽象-细化框架，通过对RNN中跨越零点的激活区间进行分区，结合SHAP引导的时间步选择策略，有效降低了验证中的松弛误差，提升了局部鲁棒性验证的成功率和紧致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: RNN的局部鲁棒性验证面临挑战：非线性松弛引入的近似误差随时间步递归传播和累积，导致线性边界传播方法过于保守，尤其在大量预激活区间跨越零点时，无法认证实际鲁棒的输入。

Method: 提出抽象-细化框架：1）对跨越零点的激活区间进行分区，在每个细化分支上ReLU变得精确，tanh/sigmoid等光滑激活函数可获得更紧的线性包络；2）引入SHAP引导的时间步选择策略，按隐藏状态对验证目标的贡献度排序，仅细化时序上最关键的时间步，以控制长序列中的组合爆炸。

Result: 在CIFAR10和MNIST stroke基准上的实验表明：相比仅用抽象的基线方法，该方法在验证成功率和鲁棒性边界的紧致性上均有持续改进，同时揭示了ReLU与tanh模型之间清晰的运行时权衡。

Conclusion: 提出的抽象-细化框架能有效提升RNN局部鲁棒性验证的精度，通过针对性细化和时间步选择策略平衡了计算代价与验证效果。

Original Abstract: Certified local robustness verification for recurrent neural networks (RNNs) is challenging because approximation errors introduced by nonlinear relaxations can propagate through recurrent connections and accumulate over time. As a result, scalable linear bound propagation methods often become overly conservative and fail to certify inputs that are in fact robust, especially when many pre-activation intervals cross zero. We propose an abstraction-refinement framework for RNN verification that partitions such intervals to remove the dominant relaxation error: on each refined branch, ReLU becomes exact, and smooth activations such as tanh and sigmoid admit substantially tighter linear envelopes. To control the combinatorial cost of splitting in long sequences, we introduce a SHAP-guided timestep selection strategy that ranks hidden states by their contribution to the verification objective and refines only the most critical timesteps in temporal order. Experiments on CIFAR10 and MNIST stroke benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in verification success and robustness-margin tightness over abstraction-only baselines, while exposing clear runtime trade-offs between ReLU and tanh models.

中文摘要翻译: 递归神经网络（RNN）的局部鲁棒性验证极具挑战性，因为非线性松弛引入的近似误差会通过递归连接传播并随时间步累积。这导致可扩展的线性边界传播方法往往过于保守，尤其在大量预激活区间跨越零点时，无法认证实际鲁棒的输入。我们提出了一种面向RNN验证的抽象-细化框架，通过对这类区间进行分区来消除主导的松弛误差：在每个细化分支上，ReLU变为精确，而tanh和sigmoid等光滑激活函数可获得显著更紧的线性包络。为控制长序列中分裂带来的组合代价，我们引入了一种SHAP引导的时间步选择策略，按隐藏状态对验证目标的贡献度进行排序，仅按时序细化最关键的时间步。在CIFAR10和MNIST stroke基准上的实验表明，相比仅使用抽象的基线方法，该方法在验证成功率和鲁棒性边界紧致性上均有持续改进，同时揭示了ReLU与tanh模型之间清晰的运行时权衡。

</details>


### [107] [Net-Ev$^2$: A Generative Simulator for Network Event Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12494)
*Guangyu Wang,Zhaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Net-Ev²，一个能够同时利用事件线索并保留网络拓扑结构的生成式模拟器，用于模拟扰动事件在现实网络中的影响传播。该框架包含结构引导的掩码预训练和拓扑感知扩散过程，在推理时仅需自然语言事件输入即可生成模拟。同时引入包含多模态基准Net-Ev²-6.5M和新评估指标JL-MMD的实验表明，该方法达到了最先进的性能并具备强泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法无法同时建模事件的结构化属性和非结构化语义，也无法在模拟网络事件演化时捕捉拓扑结构。因此，作者希望提出一种能够同时利用事件线索并保持网络拓扑的生成式模拟器。

Method: Net-Ev²包含两个阶段：1）结构引导的掩码预训练；2）拓扑感知扩散过程，该过程在去噪时通过类似U-Net的图下采样和上采样实现。推理时仅需自然语言事件输入即可生成模拟。

Result: 在四个大规模道路网络上的多模态基准Net-Ev²-6.5M上进行的实验表明，Net-Ev²达到了最先进的性能，并展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Net-Ev²能够有效模拟扰动事件在现实网络中的影响传播，其提出的拓扑感知扩散过程和拓扑保真度评估指标（JL-MMD）为网络事件演化模拟提供了新的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Reducing real-world trial and error has long been a central goal of decision making, and generative simulators advance this goal by modeling the evolution of future states. An even more challenging yet meaningful task is simulating how disturbance events (e.g., accidents) propagate their impacts across real-world networks. The existing approaches fall short of modeling both structured attributes and unstructured semantics of events, and capturing topological structures in simulating network event evolution. Therefore, we are motivated to propose Net-Ev$^2$ ($\underline{\textbf{Net}}$work $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$ent $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$olution), a novel generative simulator that jointly leverages event cues while preserving network topology in simulations. Specifically, the framework consists of two stages, namely structure-guided masked pre-training and topology-aware diffusion process, which is achieved by U-Net-like graph downsampling and upsampling during denoising. At inference time, Net-Ev$^2$ can generate simulations using natural-language event input only, with greater flexibility for practical usage. Furthermore, we introduce Net-Ev$^2$-6.5M, a multimodal benchmark of aligned event and network traffic data across four large-scale road networks, as well as a new topology-aware metric, namely JL-MMD, to evaluate topological fidelity in generated network dynamics. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization ability of Net-Ev$^2$. Code is made available at https://github.com/Guangyu4/Net-Ev-2.

中文摘要翻译: 减少现实世界中的试错一直是决策制定的核心目标，生成式模拟器通过建模未来状态演化来推进这一目标。一个更具挑战性且意义重大的任务是模拟扰动事件（如事故）如何将其影响传播到现实网络。现有方法无法同时建模事件的结构化属性和非结构化语义，也无法在模拟网络事件演化时捕捉拓扑结构。因此，我们提出Net-Ev²（$\underline{\textbf{Net}}$work $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$ent $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$olution），一种新颖的生成式模拟器，它能够在模拟中同时利用事件线索并保留网络拓扑。具体而言，该框架包含两个阶段，即结构引导的掩码预训练和拓扑感知扩散过程，后者通过在去噪过程中采用类似U-Net的图下采样和上采样来实现。在推理时，Net-Ev²仅需自然语言事件输入即可生成模拟，具有更大的实际使用灵活性。此外，我们引入了Net-Ev²-6.5M，一个包含四个大规模道路网络上对齐的事件与网络交通数据的多模态基准，以及一个新的拓扑感知度量JL-MMD，用于评估生成网络动态中的拓扑保真度。大量实验证明了Net-Ev²的最先进性能和强泛化能力。代码已开源：https://github.com/Guangyu4/Net-Ev-2。

</details>


### [108] [$μ$VLA: On Recurrent Memory for Partially Observable Manipulation in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12497)
*Egor Cherepanov,Nikita Kachaev,Daniil Zelezetsky,Aydar Bulatov,Artem Pshenitsyn,Yuri Kuratov,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控隔离实验，在强预训练VLA骨干网络中仅引入最小化的循环记忆机制（一组可学习的记忆token），验证了循环本身对部分可观测任务的有效性，并标定了其能力边界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有记忆增强型VLA模型同时引入循环、检索、压缩、辅助目标、层次记忆或任务特定架构等多种组件，使得循环本身的贡献被其他机制混淆。本文旨在通过受控隔离研究，单独评估循环机制在VLA中的真实效果。

Method: 在强预训练VLA骨干（OpenVLA-OFT）中，向Transformer添加一组可学习的记忆token，这些token跨时间步传递并通过自注意力更新，使用截断式时间反向传播（TBPTT）进行端到端训练，无需辅助损失和架构改动。由此构建μVLA模型族，参数化为记忆宽度m、TBPTT长度K和记忆更新规则（跨步梯度或解耦EMA）。

Result: 在MIKASA-Robo上，μVLA将5个训练任务的平均成功率从0.42提升至0.84（最强设置），在保留任务上达到0.23（基线0.07）；在需要不同记忆结构的任务上性能接近基线。在LIBERO上，最强循环变体达到96.2%的平均成功率，完全可观测下无性能下降。

Conclusion: 结果标定了最小化骨干内循环的能力包络：在部分可观测场景中循环足够有效，但在需要不同记忆结构的场景中仍需额外记忆机制。循环是VLA处理部分可观测性的有效且简洁的组件。

Original Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions from the current observation, an assumption that fails under partial observability, where decisions depend on information no longer visible. Existing memory-augmented VLAs simultaneously introduce recurrence, retrieval, compression modules, auxiliary objectives, hierarchical memory, or task-specific architectural changes, so the contribution of recurrence itself remains entangled with surrounding machinery. We present a controlled isolation study of recurrence in a strong pretrained VLA backbone. Our formulation augments the transformer with a small set of learnable memory tokens carried across timesteps and updated through self-attention, trained end to end with truncated backpropagation through time, with no auxiliary losses and no architectural changes. We instantiate this as $μ$VLA, a family of OpenVLA-OFT variants parameterized by memory width m, TBPTT length K, and the memory update rule (cross-step gradients or a detached EMA), so that recurrence is the only varying factor. On MIKASA-Robo, $μ$VLA improves average success rate on five training tasks from 0.42 to 0.84 at the strongest setting and reaches 0.23 on held-out tasks with the same memory structure versus 0.07 for the memoryless baseline. On tasks requiring different memory structure, performance remains near baseline. On LIBERO, the strongest recurrent variant achieves 96.2% average success, indicating no regression under full observability. We interpret these results as a calibration of the capability envelope of minimal in-backbone recurrence, identifying the regime in which it is sufficient and the regime where additional memory structure is required. Demos and videos can be found in https://avanturist322.github.io/mu-vla/.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型从当前观测预测未来动作块，这一假设在部分可观测性下会失效，因为决策依赖于已不可见的信息。现有的记忆增强型VLA模型同时引入循环、检索、压缩模块、辅助目标、层次记忆或任务特定架构变化，导致循环本身的贡献与其他机制纠缠不清。本文在一个强预训练VLA骨干中对循环机制进行了受控隔离研究。我们的方法在Transformer中增加一组可学习的记忆token，这些token跨时间步传递并通过自注意力更新，使用截断式时间反向传播（TBPTT）进行端到端训练，无需辅助损失和架构改动。我们将其实例化为μVLA——一个OpenVLA-OFT变体族，参数化为记忆宽度m、TBPTT长度K以及记忆更新规则（跨步梯度或解耦EMA），使得循环成为唯一变化的因素。在MIKASA-Robo上，μVLA在最强设置下将五个训练任务的平均成功率从0.42提升至0.84，在具有相同记忆结构的保留任务上达到0.23（无记忆基线为0.07）。在需要不同记忆结构的任务上，性能接近基线。在LIBERO上，最强循环变体实现了96.2%的平均成功率，表明在完全可观测条件下没有性能退化。我们将这些结果解释为对最小化骨干内循环能力包络的标定，识别了其足够适用的场景以及需要额外记忆结构的场景。演示和视频见https://avanturist322.github.io/mu-vla/。

</details>


### [109] [Improving Crash Frequency Prediction from Simulated Traffic Conflicts Using Machine Learning Based Microsimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12500)
*Xian Liu,Carlo G. Prato,Gustav Markkula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究将机器学习（ML）驱动的行为模型替代传统规则模型用于交通微观仿真，以提升事故预测准确性。通过在英国利兹五个信号交叉口的实验，发现ML模型生成的事故预测与实际事故数据吻合，而规则模型因缺乏针对性校准效果不佳。研究表明ML模型可真实再现冲突，但尚无法直接生成真实事故。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于微观仿真（microsimulation）的安全性研究采用简化的规则型行为模型，虽然能合理再现交通流，但难以生成真实的冲突动态，限制了事故预测精度。近年来基于机器学习的行为模型通过学习大规模轨迹数据直接模拟人类驾驶行为，有望提升仿真真实性和事故预测能力，这一可能性亟待验证。

Method: 选取英国利兹五个真实信号交叉口，分别使用标准规则模型和最先进的ML模型进行交通微观仿真。对仿真车辆轨迹采用二维碰撞时间（Two-dimensional Time-to-Collision）指标识别冲突，再基于极值理论（Extreme Value Theory, EVT）建模冲突数据以预测事故频率。最后将两种模型的事故预测结果与实际事故数据对比评估。

Result: 1）ML模型生成的冲突数据预测的事故频率与实际事故数据吻合；2）规则模型因缺乏针对特定交叉口的参数校准，无法产生有意义的预测；3）直接使用ML模型生成的仿真事故来预测真实事故频率效果较差，表明当前ML模型虽能真实再现冲突，但尚无法生成真实事故。

Conclusion: 基于ML的行为模型在从仿真冲突预测事故方面具有明显优势，且无需针对特定地点进行模型校准。研究结果为ML驱动的交通微观仿真指明了清晰的发展方向。

Original Abstract: Traffic microsimulation combined with surrogate safety measures has increasingly been used as a proactive alternative to historical crash data for predicting crash frequency for current or planned road infrastructure designs. However, existing microsimulation-based safety studies have adopted simplified rule-based behaviour models, which reproduce traffic flow reasonably well but often fail to generate realistic conflict dynamics, limiting crash prediction accuracy. Recent advances in machine learning (ML)-based behaviour models offer a promising opportunity to potentially improve microsimulation realism and crash frequency predictions by learning human driving behaviour directly from large-scale trajectory datasets. To investigate this possibility, traffic microsimulation was conducted for five real-world signalised intersections in Leeds, UK, using both a standard rule-based model and a state-of-the-art ML model. Simulated vehicle trajectories were analysed using a two-dimensional Time-to-Collision metric to identify simulated conflicts, which were then modelled using Extreme Value Theory to predict crash frequency. Results show that conflicts from the ML model yielded crash predictions in line with the real-world crash data, whereas the rule-based model did not permit meaningful predictions, presumably due to a lack of model calibration to the specific simulated intersections. Directly using ML-generated simulated crashes to predict real-world crash frequency also yielded poor results, suggesting that while current ML models can realistically reproduce conflicts, they are not yet able to generate realistic crashes. Overall, the findings demonstrate that ML-based behaviour models are promising for improving crash prediction from simulated conflicts, without a need for location-specific model calibration, and suggest clear future directions for ML-based traffic microsimulation.

中文摘要翻译: 交通微观仿真与替代性安全度量相结合，已被越来越多地用作一种主动替代方案，利用历史事故数据来预测当前或规划道路基础设施设计的事故频率。然而，现有基于微观仿真的安全性研究采用了简化的规则型行为模型，这些模型虽能较好地再现交通流，但通常无法生成真实的冲突动态，从而限制了事故预测精度。近期机器学习（ML）行为模型的进展提供了一种有前景的机遇——通过直接从大规模轨迹数据中学习人类驾驶行为，有望提升微观仿真的真实性和事故频率预测能力。为探究这一可能性，我们选取英国利兹五个真实信号交叉口，分别使用标准规则模型和最先进的ML模型进行交通微观仿真。利用二维碰撞时间（Time-to-Collision）指标分析仿真车辆轨迹，识别仿真冲突，进而采用极值理论（Extreme Value Theory）对冲突建模以预测事故频率。结果表明：基于ML模型的冲突数据得出的事故预测与实际事故数据吻合，而规则模型因缺乏针对特定仿真交叉口的模型校准，无法产生有意义的预测。直接利用ML模型生成的仿真事故来预测真实事故频率效果也不佳，表明当前ML模型虽能真实再现冲突，但尚无法生成真实事故。总体而言，研究结果证明基于ML的行为模型在无需针对特定地点进行模型校准的情况下，能够有效提升基于仿真冲突的事故预测准确性，并为基于ML的交通微观仿真指明了清晰的发展方向。

</details>


### [110] [Out-of-Distribution (OOD) Detectors for Open-Set RF Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12718)
*Sudeepta Mondal,Ganesh Sundaramoorthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对射频指纹识别（RFF）在开放世界场景中面临未知发射机和时变漂移导致的分布偏移问题，引入基于信息论统一框架的OOD检测方法，并证明了无需辅助OOD数据即可调优检测器的可行性，在POWDER数据集上取得了与使用真实OOD数据调优的基线相当的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 射频指纹识别系统需要在开放世界环境中运行，测试时会遇到未知发射机信号和时变漂移导致的分布偏移。OOD检测为此问题提供了自然框架，但其在RFF中的应用仍然有限。主要障碍在于大多数OOD检测器需要辅助OOD数据进行参数调优，而在射频环境中收集代表性的OOD数据并不现实。

Method: 本文从机器学习文献中引入一系列有前景的OOD检测方法到开放集RFF领域，并基于信息论构建统一的数学框架，实现对方法的系统分析和新方法的开发。同时，采用无需给定OOD调优数据的调优技术，使其适用于开放集RFF场景。

Result: 在POWDER射频指纹识别数据集上的实验表明：无需任何给定OOD数据调优的检测器，其性能与能够访问真实OOD调优数据的基线方法相当，且大幅优于没有真实OOD调优数据的基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在射频指纹识别问题中，无需依赖难以获取的真实OOD数据即可有效调优OOD检测器，展示了该方法在RFF问题中的实际可行性。

Original Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) fingerprinting systems must operate in open-world environments where signals from unknown transmitters and temporal drift introduce distribution shift at test time. Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection provides a natural framework for this problem, yet its application to RF fingerprinting (RFF) remains limited. A key barrier to their adoption is that most OOD detectors require auxiliary OOD data for parameter tuning, an assumption that is difficult to satisfy in RF environments where representative OOD data is impractical to collect. In this work, we introduce a promising set of OOD detection methods from the machine learning literature to open-set RFF domain. We present these methods within a unified mathematical framework based on information theory, which is a natural framework for communication systems. Our framework allows for the systematic analysis of methods and development of new methods. We further demonstrate the applicability of recent work on tuning OOD detectors without given OOD tuning data for open-set RFF. We evaluate on the POWDER RF fingerprinting dataset, showing that detectors tuned without any given OOD data achieve performance comparable to baselines with access to true OOD tuning data and greatly out-perform baseline approaches without access to true OOD tuning data, showcasing the practical viability for the RFF problem.

中文摘要翻译: 射频指纹识别系统必须在开放世界环境中运行，在此环境中，来自未知发射机的信号以及时变漂移会在测试时引起分布偏移。分布外检测为此问题提供了自然框架，但其在射频指纹识别中的应用仍然有限。采用分布外检测方法的一个关键障碍是，大多数分布外检测器需要辅助的分布外数据进行参数调优，而在射频环境中，这一假设难以满足，因为收集具有代表性的分布外数据并不可行。在本工作中，我们将机器学习文献中一系列有前景的分布外检测方法引入到开放集射频指纹识别领域。我们基于信息论构建了统一的数学框架来呈现这些方法，信息论是通信系统的自然理论基础。该框架允许我们对方法进行系统分析并开发新方法。我们进一步展示了近期关于无需给定分布外调优数据即可调优分布外检测器的工作在开放集射频指纹识别中的适用性。我们在POWDER射频指纹识别数据集上进行了评估，结果表明：无需任何给定分布外数据调优的检测器，其性能与能够访问真实分布外调优数据的基线方法相当，且大幅优于没有真实分布外调优数据访问权限的基线方法，这展示了该方法在射频指纹识别问题中的实际可行性。

</details>


### [111] [Policy-driven Conformal Prediction for Trustworthy QoT Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12501)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Omran Ayoub,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Conformal QoT框架，结合统计保证的QoT估计与操作决策策略，实现域偏移下可靠的光路可行性预测，将准确率从92%提升至99.6%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有光路可行性预测方法在域偏移（domain shift）场景下性能下降严重，缺乏统计保证的可靠估计。

Method: 提出Conformal QoT框架，这是一种策略驱动方法，将具有统计保证的QoT估计与操作决策策略相结合。

Result: 在开放数据集上，将光路可行性预测准确率从92%提升至99.6%。

Conclusion: Conformal QoT框架能够有效应对域偏移，实现高可靠性的光路可行性预测。

Original Abstract: We propose Conformal QoT, a policy-driven framework that combines statistically guaranteed QoT estimation with operational decision policies, enabling reliable lightpath-feasibility predictions under domain shift and improving accuracy from 92\% to 99.6\% on open datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了Conformal QoT，一种策略驱动的框架，它将具有统计保证的QoT估计与操作决策策略相结合，能够在域偏移下实现可靠的光路可行性预测，并在开放数据集上将准确率从92%提升至99.6%。

</details>


### [112] [Dolph2Vec: Self-Supervised Representations of Dolphin Vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12503)
*Chiara Semenzin,Faadil Mustun,Roberto Dessi,Pierre Orhan,Alexis Emanuelli,Yair Lakretz,Gonzalo de Polavieja,German Sumbre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dolph2Vec，首个针对特定物种（海豚）的大规模自监督学习模型，基于五年纵向录音数据训练，在海豚哨声分类和检测任务上显著优于通用基线，并展示了自监督学习在动物沟通研究中的科学工具潜力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的生物声学自监督学习模型侧重于跨物种的广泛泛化，未针对个体动物沟通系统的细粒度结构进行优化，缺乏对特定物种（如海豚）长期通信模式深入分析的能力。

Method: 收集并发布了超过五年、来自五只已知海豚的纵向录音数据集；基于Wav2Vec2.0架构进行领域适配，训练了首个大规模物种特异性自监督学习模型Dolph2Vec。

Result: 在签名哨声分类和哨声检测两项生物学相关任务上，Dolph2Vec显著优于通用基线；学习到的嵌入和码本结构能够捕获与海豚哨声类别对应的可解释声学单元，甚至可能包含亚哨声结构。

Conclusion: 自监督学习可以作为动物沟通研究中的模型和科学工具，用于探索假设并进行细粒度分析。

Original Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has opened new opportunities in bioacoustics by enabling scalable modeling of animal vocalizations without the need for expensive manual annotation. However, current SSL models in this domain prioritize broad generalization across species and are not optimized for uncovering the fine-grained structure of individual communication systems. In this work, we collect and release a novel dataset of over five years of longitudinal recordings, from five known dolphins in a semi-naturalistic marine environment, an unprecedented resource for studying dolphin communication. We adapt the Wav2Vec2.0 Baevski et al. (2020) architecture to this domain and introduce Dolph2Vec, the first large-scale, species-specific SSL model trained exclusively on this data. We benchmark our model on two biologically relevant tasks: signature whistle classification and whistle detection. Dolph2Vec significantly outperforms general-purpose baselines in both tasks. Beyond performance, we show that learned embeddings and codebook structure capture interpretable acoustic units aligned with dolphin whistle categories and possibly sub-whistle structure, enabling fine-grained analysis of communication patterns. Our findings demonstrate how SSL can serve as both a model and a scientific tool to explore hypotheses in animal communication research.

中文摘要翻译: 自监督学习（SSL）为生物声学领域开辟了新的机遇，使得无需昂贵的人工标注即可对动物发声进行可扩展建模。然而，当前该领域的SSL模型侧重于跨物种的广泛泛化，并未针对揭示个体沟通系统的细粒度结构进行优化。本研究收集并发布了一个新颖的数据集，包含来自半自然海洋环境中五只已知海豚的五年以上纵向录音，这是研究海豚沟通的前所未有的资源。我们将Wav2Vec2.0（Baevski et al., 2020）架构适配到该领域，并提出了Dolph2Vec——首个仅在此数据上训练的大规模物种特异性SSL模型。我们在两个生物学相关任务上对模型进行基准测试：签名哨声分类和哨声检测。Dolph2Vec在这两项任务上均显著优于通用基线。除了性能提升，我们还展示了学习到的嵌入和码本结构能够捕获与海豚哨声类别对应的可解释声学单元，甚至可能包含亚哨声结构，从而实现对沟通模式的细粒度分析。我们的发现证明了SSL如何作为模型和科学工具，用于探索动物沟通研究中的假设。

</details>


### [113] [Boosting Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12505)
*Pengwei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DPOP，通过在DPO损失上增加一个门控惩罚项，利用参考模型自身的生成响应来改进离线偏好优化，在AlpacaEval 2.0上相比DPO、SimPO和AlphaDPO取得了显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有离线偏好优化方法（如DPO及其变体）仅使用了静态数据集中存储的选择和拒绝响应，忽略了参考模型对同一提示自身生成的响应这一有用的信号。

Method: 提出DPOP（Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization），在DPO基础偏好损失上增加一个门控惩罚项。该惩罚项仅在当前策略对偏好响应的似然仍低于对拒绝响应的似然时激活，惩罚参考模型的贪心响应。

Result: 在AlpacaEval 2.0上，DPOP在Llama-3-8b-it和Gemma-2-9b-it上分别比基线方法（DPO、SimPO、AlphaDPO）提升了5.3%和4.4%的长度控制胜率。消融实验表明，SimNPO风格的长度归一化惩罚优于NPO和词级unlikelihood方法。

Conclusion: 利用参考模型自身生成响应作为额外信号，通过门控惩罚机制可以有效提升离线偏好优化的性能，且长度归一化惩罚策略效果最佳。

Original Abstract: Offline preference optimization has become a practical substitute for reinforcement learning from human feedback, but pairwise objectives such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and its variants use only the chosen and rejected responses stored in a static dataset. This leaves a useful signal unused: the response that the reference model itself would generate for the same prompt. We propose Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization (DPOP), a simple extension of DPO that augments the base preference loss with a gated penalty on reference-greedy responses. DPOP activates this penalty only when the current policy still assigns a lower likelihood to the preferred response than to the rejected response. On AlpacaEval 2.0, DPOP improves length-controlled win rate over DPO, SimPO, and AlphaDPO on both Llama-3-8b-it and Gemma-2-9b-it, achieving relative gains of 5.3\% and 4.4\% over baselines on the two models, respectively. Ablations further show that a SimNPO-style length-normalized penalty is stronger than NPO and token-level unlikelihood in this setting.

中文摘要翻译: 离线偏好优化已成为从人类反馈中进行强化学习的实用替代方案，但诸如直接偏好优化（DPO）及其变体等成对目标函数仅使用了存储在静态数据集中的选择和拒绝响应。这留下了一个有用的信号未被利用：参考模型本身对同一提示生成的响应。我们提出了带惩罚的直接偏好优化（DPOP），这是DPO的一个简单扩展，它在基础偏好损失上增加了一个针对参考贪心响应的门控惩罚。DPOP仅在当前策略对偏好响应的似然仍然低于对拒绝响应的似然时激活该惩罚。在AlpacaEval 2.0上，DPOP在Llama-3-8b-it和Gemma-2-9b-it上相比DPO、SimPO和AlphaDPO均提升了长度控制胜率，在两个模型上分别比基线方法获得了5.3%和4.4%的相对增益。消融实验进一步表明，在该设定下，SimNPO风格的长度归一化惩罚优于NPO和词级unlikelihood方法。

</details>


### [114] [Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation: Post-Training Without Rubric Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12507)
*MohammadHossein Rezaei,Anas Mahmoud,Zihao Wang,Utkarsh Tyagi,Advait Gosai,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Aakash Sabharwal,Bing Liu,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Rubric引导的自蒸馏方法（RGSD），在无需验证器的情况下实现与基于评判器的GRPO相当的评分满意度，同时完全消除训练时的验证器开销。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于rubric的训练方法依赖LLM验证器对每次推演打分，带来训练开销大、受验证器偏差影响、反馈稀疏等问题。

Method: RGSD是一种无验证器的训练方法：将基础策略在rubric条件下的分布作为教师，蒸馏到无条件的student模型中，实现逐token的密集学习信号替代稀疏的轨迹级奖励，完全移除LLM评判器。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5（3B, 7B）和Qwen3-Thinking（4B, 8B）的医学和科学领域上，RGSD仅用每提示一次on-policy rollout且无需训练时验证器调用，即可达到与基于评判器的GRPO相当的rubric满意度。消融实验表明原始rubric比自生成参考回答提供更强的教师富化信号，但强GRPO评判器在某些设置下仍优于RGSD。

Conclusion: RGSD可作为验证器成本和可靠性成为瓶颈时的互补性无验证器替代方案。

Original Abstract: Rubrics have emerged as an alternative to RLVR in open-ended domains where a single ground-truth final answer is not available. Existing rubric-based training methods rely on an LLM verifier that scores each rollout against rubrics. This introduces substantial training-time overhead, exposes optimization to verifier-specific biases, and reduces rubric feedback to a sparse end-of-trajectory signal. We propose Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation (RGSD), a verifier-free training method in which the base policy, conditioned on the rubric, serves as the teacher for the unconditioned student. RGSD distills the rubric-conditioned teacher distribution into the student token-by-token, replacing sparse trajectory-level rewards with dense per-token learning signals and removing the LLM judge from the training loop entirely. Across Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B) and Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B) models on medical and science domains, RGSD achieves rubric satisfaction comparable to judge-based GRPO while using one on-policy rollout per prompt and no training-time verifier calls. Ablations show that raw rubrics provide a stronger teacher enrichment signal than self-generated reference responses, while a stronger GRPO judge can outperform RGSD in some settings, positioning RGSD as a complementary verifier-free alternative when verifier cost or reliability is the bottleneck.

中文摘要翻译: 在无法获得单一真实正确答案的开放领域，评分准则已成为RLVR的替代方案。现有基于评分准则的训练方法依赖一个LLM验证器，该验证器根据评分准则对每次推演进行打分。这引入了大量训练时间开销，使优化过程暴露于验证器特定的偏差，并将评分准则反馈简化为稀疏的轨迹末端信号。我们提出了评分准则引导的自蒸馏（RGSD），一种无验证器的训练方法，其中以评分准则为条件的基础策略作为教师，服务于无条件的student模型。RGSD将评分准则条件下的教师分布逐token蒸馏到student中，用密集的逐token学习信号取代稀疏的轨迹级奖励，并将LLM评判器完全从训练循环中移除。在Qwen-2.5（3B, 7B）和Qwen3-Thinking（4B, 8B）模型上，针对医学和科学领域，RGSD在仅使用每提示一次on-policy rollout且无需训练时验证器调用的情况下，达到了与基于评判器的GRPO相当的评分准则满意度。消融实验表明，原始评分准则比自生成的参考回答提供了更强的教师富化信号，而更强的GRPO评判器在某些设置下可以优于RGSD，这使RGSD成为在验证器成本或可靠性成为瓶颈时的一种互补性无验证器替代方案。

</details>


### [115] [Crossing the Validation Crisis: Cross-Validation Reduces Benchmarking Variance Surprisingly Well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12552)
*Célestin Eve,Gaël Varoquaux,Thomas Moreau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出机器学习评估中因测试样本有限和算法随机性导致的统计变异性问题，提出使用多重交叉验证来提升置信度，引入“样本增益”概念量化虚拟数据增强效果，并设计动态早停策略以在有限样本下实现稳健可靠的基准测试。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代机器学习依赖经验性基准测试来评估方法性能，但由于测试样本有限以及许多算法的随机性，性能估计往往不可靠，导致难以区分真正的进展，存在验证危机。

Method: 1) 提出“样本增益”概念，量化多重交叉验证分割所实现的虚拟数据增强效果，用于降低基准测试方差；2) 设计动态早停交叉验证流程，通过前几折的估计判断后续折是否会带来较大的样本增益，从而提前终止交叉验证。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据集（组织病理学扫描和NLP微调）上的实验表明，多重分割能显著提升性能估计的可靠性和稳定性，且收益递减点往往比预期来得更晚。

Conclusion: 强调在有限样本上充分利用交叉验证的价值，以实现稳健可靠的基准测试。

Original Abstract: Modern machine learning progresses through empirical work, benchmarking new methods to evaluate relative performance. However, the statistical variability inherent to evaluation - exacerbated by the stochastic nature of many algorithms - often makes performance estimation unreliable due to the limited test samples available, leading to a validation crisis in which genuine advances are difficult to discern. In this work, we show that cross-validation improves markedly confidence when evaluating and comparing learning algorithm performances. We introduce the concept of sample gain, which quantifies the virtual data augmentation achieved by using multiple cross-validation splits to reduce benchmarking variance. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets (histopathologic scans and NLP fine-tuning) demonstrate that multiple splits can substantially improve the reliability and stability of performance estimates, with diminishing returns often setting in later than expected. We also introduce a procedure to dynamically early-stop cross-validation by estimating from the first few folds if subsequent folds will bring large sample gains. Our findings highlight the value of pushing cross-validation on available samples to achieve robust and reliable benchmarking.

中文摘要翻译: 现代机器学习通过实证工作推进，通过基准测试新方法来评估相对性能。然而，评估中固有的统计变异性——由于许多算法的随机性而加剧——常常因有限的测试样本导致性能估计不可靠，从而引发验证危机，使得难以辨别真正的进展。在这项工作中，我们展示了交叉验证在评估和比较学习算法性能时能显著提升置信度。我们引入了样本增益的概念，该概念量化了通过使用多重交叉验证分割来减少基准测试方差所实现的虚拟数据增强效果。在合成数据和真实世界数据集（组织病理学扫描和NLP微调）上的实验表明，多重分割可以显著提高性能估计的可靠性和稳定性，且收益递减点往往比预期来得更晚。我们还引入了一种流程，通过从前几折估计后续折是否会带来较大的样本增益，来动态早停交叉验证。我们的发现强调了在可用样本上充分利用交叉验证以实现稳健可靠基准测试的价值。

</details>


### [116] [Emerging Flexible Designs for Geospatial Multimodal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12595)
*Philipe Dias,Waqwoya Abebe,Abhishek Potnis,Aristeidis Tsaris,Dan Lu,Xiao Wang,Dalton Lunga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对多种主流地理空间基础模型架构（编码器-only、编码器-解码器、掩码自编码）进行了公平对比，在统一的自监督学习目标、训练数据集和参数化条件下，评估了它们在多模态推理和不同光谱波段配置下的性能，揭示了模型灵活性、模态对齐与下游任务表现之间的设计权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有地理空间基础模型架构多样（编码器-only、编码器-解码器、掩码自编码等），缺乏在统一条件下对其性能权衡进行一致评估的研究，因此需要开展公平的对比分析以指导模型设计。

Method: 采用苹果对苹果（apples-to-apples）的对比方法，标准化所有模型的预训练过程，使用相同的自监督学习目标和训练数据集，在一致的参数化条件下在GEOBench基准上评估分类和分割任务。

Result: 研究结果揭示了模型灵活性、模态对齐与下游任务性能之间的设计权衡，展示了不同架构在受控条件下的优势与局限性。

Conclusion: 通过突出不同架构在受控条件下的优势与局限，本研究为构建能够进行鲁棒多模态推理的下一代地理空间基础模型提供了实用指导。

Original Abstract: Foundation models are rapidly transforming Earth observation by enabling scalable pretraining across diverse unlabeled geospatial modalities. However, their architectural diversity ranging from encoder-only to encoder-decoder and masked autoencoding paradigms makes it challenging to assess performance trade offs in a consistent manner. In this work, we present an apples-to-apples comparison of leading FM architectures designed for geospatial multimodal reasoning, with a particular focus on flexibility across varied spectral band configurations. We standardize pretraining using identical self supervised learning objectives and training datasets, and evaluate all models under consistent parameterization on the GEOBench benchmark across classification and segmentation tasks. Our results offer new insights into the design trade-offs between model flexibility, modality alignment, and downstream task performance. By highlighting architectural strengths and limitations under controlled conditions, this study provides practical guidance for building next generation geospatial foundation models capable of robust multimodal reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 基础模型正通过跨多样未标注地理空间模态的可扩展预训练，迅速改变地球观测领域。然而，从编码器-only到编码器-解码器以及掩码自编码范式，这些模型的架构多样性使得以一致方式评估性能权衡变得具有挑战性。在这项工作中，我们针对专为地理空间多模态推理设计的主流基础模型架构进行了苹果对苹果的公平对比，特别关注了跨不同光谱波段配置的灵活性。我们使用相同的自监督学习目标和训练数据集标准化预训练过程，并在一致的参数化条件下，在GEOBench基准上对所有模型在分类和分割任务中进行了评估。我们的研究结果揭示了模型灵活性、模态对齐与下游任务性能之间的设计权衡。通过突出不同架构在受控条件下的优势与局限，本研究为构建能够进行鲁棒多模态推理的下一代地理空间基础模型提供了实用指导。

</details>


### [117] [Viral Proteins Reveal Geometry of Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12609)
*Arthur Bigot,Harmon Bhasin,Core Francisco Park,Eugene Shakhnovich,Dianzhuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究蛋白质语言模型如何处理不平衡数据中欠表示的生物序列（以病毒蛋白为例），发现嵌入空间中存在一个主导的“自然性”轴，该轴通过掩码重建困惑度将序列从细胞蛋白到病毒蛋白再到随机序列排序，且模型缩放会不均匀地压缩这一轴；尽管存在这种结构，嵌入仍保留病毒特异性信息，使其线性可分。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型在不平衡数据集上训练，导致对欠表示生物序列（如病毒蛋白）的表征方式不明确，因此需要探究模型如何处理这些序列。

Method: 以病毒蛋白为案例，在ESM模型家族中分析嵌入空间的结构，使用掩码重建困惑度作为排序指标，并进行线性可分性分析。

Result: 1）嵌入空间中存在一条主导的“自然性”轴，按细胞蛋白→病毒蛋白→打乱/随机序列的顺序排列；2）模型缩放不均匀地压缩不同病毒家族的轴间距；3）病毒蛋白在零样本困惑度和浅层序列特征之外仍保持线性可分性。

Conclusion: 蛋白质语言模型的表征由一种广义的“自然性”概念所结构化，同时保留了对不同生物群体的特异性信息。

Original Abstract: Protein language models are trained on highly imbalanced datasets, raising the question of how they represent underrepresented biological sequences. Using viral proteins as a case study across ESM model families, we identify a dominant nativeness axis in embedding space, aligned with masked reconstruction perplexity, that orders sequences from well-modeled cellular proteins through viral proteins to shuffled and random sequences. Scaling contracts this axis unevenly across viral families. Despite this, protein language model embeddings retain viral-specific signal: viral proteins remain linearly separable beyond zero-shot perplexity and shallow sequence features. Together, these results suggest that pLM representations are structured by a general notion of nativeness while preserving information specific to distinct biological groups.
